patippurai
tamiḻukkum tamiḻarukkum vaḷamum valimaiyum cērkkak
kūṭiya paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷaiyellām tēṭiyeṭuttut tamiḻ kūṟum ulakiṟku vaḻaṅkum tolainōkkup pārvaiyuṭaṉ  tamiḻmaṇ patippakam toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. ataṉ vāyilāka moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇariṉ nūṟṟāṇṭu niṉaivāka avar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ aṉaittaiyum cērttu avaruṭaiya maṟaivukkup piṟaku 2000-ttilum, palvēṟu itaḻkaḷilum, malarkaḷilum veḷivantu, nūl vaṭivam peṟāta pāvāṇariṉ ariya kaṭṭuraikaḷai ellām tokuttu 2001- lum orucēra veḷiyiṭṭu ulakeṅkum uḷḷa tamiḻarkaḷukku vaḻaṅkiṉōm.  pāvāṇar vaḻi nilai aṟiñarkaḷāṉa mutumuṉaivar 
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷum, maruttuvar ku.pūṅkāvaṉam avarkaḷum ivvariya kaṭṭuraip putaiyalkaḷ nūl vaṭivam peṟuvataṟku mikavum uṟutuṇaiyāka iruntaṉar. ipperumakkaḷai naṉṟiyuṇarvuṭaṉ niṉaivukūrkiṟēṉ.
collārāyccit tuṟaiyil tēvanēyar oppaṟṟa taṉit
tiṟamaiyuṭaivar eṉṟu maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷum, nāvalan tīvukku  nantamiḻē tāyeṉṟu pāvēntarum, tamiḻukkum tamiḻ
nāṭṭukkum naṟṟoṇṭu āṟṟiyavar pāvāṇar eṉṟu pēraṟiñar aṇṇā avarkaḷum, veṭṭa veṭṭak kiṭaikkum taṅkac
curaṅkam pōṉṟavar eṉṟu tamiḻiṟiñar irācamāṇikkaṉārum, tamiḻakam moḻittuṟaiyilē pāvāṇar pōṉṟa oru aṟiñarai iṉṉum peṟṟut taravillai eṉṟu paṉmoḻip pulavar
appātturaiyarum, kuṟaimatiyar tēkkivaitta karaiyiruḷai nīkka vanta maṟaimalaiyār vaḻivanta niṟaimalaiyār pāvāṇar eṉṟu mēṉāḷ pēravaittalaivar tamiḻkuṭimakaṉ avarkaḷum, tamiḻar yār? etirikaḷ yār? eṉṟu āyntu aṟintu kāṭṭiyavar pāvāṇar eṉṟu pērāciriyar iḷavaracu avarkaḷum,  ōrāyiram āṇṭukaḷ ōyntu kiṭantapiṉ vārātu pōla vanta māmaṇi pāvāṇar eṉṟu mutumuṉaivar iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷa  perumaikaḷukkuriya pēraṟiñariṉ nūlkaḷai mīḷpatippaka veḷiyiṭuvatil perumai koḷkiṟēṉ.
pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ attaṉaiyum tamiḻmoḻikku ēṟṟam uraippaṉa. tamiḻai āriya iruḷiṉiṉṟu mīṭṭuk kāppaṉa. vīḻntu
paṭṭa tamiḻaṉukku viḻippūṭṭa vallaṉa. āyiramāyiram āṇṭukaḷāka aṭimaippaṭṭukkiṭakkum tamiḻmoḻiyai mīṭkavallatu eṉṟu  pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār avarkaḷiṉ kūṟṟai ik kaḷañciya veḷiyīṭṭil pativu ceyvatu ematu kaṭamaiyākum.
pāvāṇarait tūkkip piṭittāltāṉ tamiḻiṉam urup
paṭamuṭiyum - uyaramuṭiyum. pāvāṇar koḷkaikaḷ tamiḻar uḷḷamellām nilaittu niṟpataṟkum, pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ tamiḻar illamelām iṭam peṟuvataṟkum muṉ kuṟippiṭṭa 2000 - 2001 kālakkaṭṭattil veḷiyiṭappaṭṭa pāvāṇariṉ aṟivuk karuvūlaṅkaḷaiyellām oṭṭu mottamāka cērttu orē vīccil pāvāṇar tamiḻk kaḷañciyam eṉum talaippil  em aṟakkaṭṭaḷai veḷiyiṭukiṟatu.
maṟaikkappeṟṟa māperum varalāṟṟaiyum, citaikkap peṟṟa oppuyarvuyaṟṟa moḻiyaiyum koṇṭa tamiḻiṉattiṉ muṉṉēṟṟam karuti ivvaruntamiḻ putaiyalkaḷai tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakiṟku  vaḻaṅka muṉ vantuḷḷōm. tamiḻ moḻiyai mūccākavum, pēccākavum, ceyalākavum koṇṭu orunāḷiṉ muḻuppoḻutum tamiḻākavē vāḻnta cempulaccemmal,  taṉittamiḻk katiravaṉ moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar nūlkaḷai vāṅkip payaṉ koḷvīr.
iḷamaiyilēyē potuttoṇṭilum, taṉittamiḻ iyakkat toṇṭilum nāṉ īṭupāṭu koḷvataṟkuk kāraṇamāka iruntavarkaḷ perumpulavar nakkīraṉ avarkaḷum, avartam iḷaval pulavar cittiravēlaṉ avarkaḷum āvar. ivarkaḷai ivveḷiyiṭṭiṉ vāyilāka naṉṟiyuṇarvōṭu niṉaivu kūṟukiṟēṉ.  tantai periyāriṉ taṉmatippu iyakkak koḷkai
kaḷālum, moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇariṉ taṉittamiḻ iyakkak koḷkaikaḷālum īrkkappaṭṭavaṉ. attaku piṉpulattōṭu patippuppaṇiyil eṉ kālaṭic cuvaṭukaḷaip patittu varukiṟēṉ.
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ,
patippāḷar.

kuṟi viḷakkam
+	itu puṇarkuṟi
=	itu camakkuṟi
-	itu valappuṟattiluḷḷatu moḻipeyarppu, poruḷ, tiripu eṉṉum mūṉṟaṉuḷ oṉṟu eṉpataik kuṟikkum
<	itu valappuṟattiluḷḷatu mūlam eṉpataik kuṟikkum
x	itu etirmaṟaik kuṟi
	itu mēṟkōṭ kuṟiyallāviṭattu mēṟpaṭikkuṟi
( )	itu cila iṭaṅkaḷil vaḻimuṟait tirivaik kuṟikkum.
	vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa ‘ri’ eṉṉum uyireḻuttu, ip puttakattil 
‘ru’ eṉṟeḻutappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.
ip puttakattiṟkup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa āṅkilac colliyalakarātikaḷ kīṟṟum (Skeat) cēmparārum (Chambers) eḻutiyavai. āṅkāṅkut tolkāppiya nūṟpāviṟkuk kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa eṇ, cai. ci. nū. pa. ka. acciṭṭa tolkāppiya mūlattiṉpaṭiyatu.

oppiyaṉ moḻinūl - 2

2
paṇṭait tamiḻakam
aḻintupōṉa tamiḻnūlkaḷ
ēṉaiya moḻikaḷilellām ilakkiyam varavara uyarntum mikuntum varavum, tamiḻilō varavarat tāḻntum kuṟaintum vantirukkiṉṟatu. vaṭamoḻi teṉmoḻi yilakkiyaṅkaḷ iru peruṅkaṭalkaḷākat toṉṉūlkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, vaṭamoḻikkaṭal muṉṉuḷḷapaṭiyē iṉṟum kuṟaiyātuḷatu. āṉāl, teṉmoḻikkaṭalō oru ciṟu kuḷamāka vaṟṟiyuḷḷatu.
akattiyarukku muntiya tamiḻnūlkaḷil, ceṅkōṉ taraiccelavu eṉṉum ciṟiya nūliṉ oru pakutiyē iṉṟu kiṭaittuḷatu.
talaikkaḻakattārāṟ pāṭappaṭṭa ettuṇaiyō paripāṭalum, mutunāraiyum mutukurukum, kaḷariyāviraiyumeṉa ittoṭakkattaṉavum iṭaikkaḻakattārāṟ pāṭappaṭṭa kaliyum, kurukum, veṇṭāḷiyum, viyāḻamālaiyakava lumeṉa it toṭakkattaṉavum kaṭaikkaḻakattārāṟ pāṭappaṭṭa paripāṭaluṭ palavum, kūttum variyum ciṟṟicaiyum, pēricaiyum ip pōtillai.
talaikkaḻakakkālat tilakkaṇamākiya akattiyamum, iṭaik kaḻakat tilakkaṇamākiya māpurāṇamum icainūlum pūta purāṇamum ippōtillai.

pāṇṭiyaṉ tamiḻppāratam, tiruccirāppaḷḷiyantāti, srīrāja rāja vijayam, nāṭakanūl, vīraṇukka vijayam, kulōttuṅka cōḻa caritai, aṣṭātaca purāṇam, aruṇilai vicākaṉ tamiḻp pāratam, peruvañci, attikirit tirumāl cintu, kāṅkēyaṉ piḷḷaikkavi, vīramālai, tiruvatikaik kalampakam, tiru vallaiyantāti mutaliyavai, ituvarai yaṟiyappaṭāta nūlkaḷākac cāsaṉat tamiḻkkavi caritattiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
kaṭaikkaḻakattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭa pulavarkaḷiyaṟṟiya nūlkaḷiṟ pala ippōtu kiṭaikkavillai. oṭṭakkūttariyaṟṟiya arumpait toḷḷāyiram, etirnūl mutaliyavai iṉṉum veḷi varavillai.
iṭaikkaḻaka virukkaiyākiya kapāṭapurattil, eṇṇā yirat teccam nūlkaḷiruntu piṉpu kaṭalkōḷuṇṭaḻintaṉa veṉṟoru vaḻimuṟai vaḻakkuḷḷatu.

akattiyar
akattiyar eṉṟu ettaṉaiyō pēruḷar. avaruḷ mutalāvārē iṅkuk kūṟappaṭupavar.
akattiyar kālam
akattiyar irāmar kālattavarātaliṉ, ēṟattāḻak ki.mu. 1200 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭavarāvar.
akattiyar kataikaḷ
(1) vintattiṉ cerukkaṭakkiṉatu
orukālattil paṉimalai (imayam) nīrkkīḻ iruntatu. appōtu vintiyamalai mikavuyaramāyt tōṉṟiṟṟu. paṉimalai yeḻuntapiṉ, ataṉoṭu oppunōkka vintam mikac ciṟitāyt tōṉṟiṟṟu. itē vintattiṉ cerukkaṭakkam. itai akattiyar peyaroṭu btāṭuttukTṟakfraṇam,mvarbtaṉṉāṭutntapiṉFmarināṭṭaikfllfṇṭamaiyē.af°âa® eṉṉum peyarukku, vintattiṉ akattai (cerukkai) aṭakki ṉavar eṉṟu, vaṭamoḻiyiṟ poruḷ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
(2) kaṭalaik kuṭittatu
muṉpu kaṭalāyirunta pākam piṉpu nilamākip paṉimalai tōṉṟiyataiyē, akattiyar kaṭalaik kuṭittatākak katai kaṭṭiyatākat terikiṉṟatu.

kuṭṭai - kuṭṭam. o.nō paṭṭai - paṭṭam, taṭṭai - taṭṭam.
ceyyuḷaṭikaḷ kuṟaḷuñ cintumāyk kuṟuki varuvatu kuṭṭam paṭutal eṉṟum, kaikāl viralkaḷ aḻukik kuṭṭaiyākum 
toḻunōyum kuraṅkukkuṭṭiyuṅ kuṭṭameṉṟuṅ kūṟappaṭutal kāṇka.
kuṭṭattaik kuṣṭameṉpar vaṭanūlār. o.nō. nfh£l« nfhZl« (t.), muṭṭi - muṣṭi (va.).
cila peyarkaḷil, am eṉṉum muṉṉoṭṭuc cērtal iyalpu.
kā: aṅkayaṟkaṇṇi, aṅkāḷammai.
iṅṅaṉamē kuṭṭamuṉi eṉṉum peyarum aṅkuṭṭamuṉi eṉṟu ākiyirukkalām.
aṅkuṭṭaṉ eṉṟu ēṟkeṉavē peruviralukkup peyar. kuṭṭaiyāyiruppatāl kaṭṭaiviral kuṭṭaṉ eṉappaṭṭatu. aḵṟiṇaip peyarum aṉ īṟu peṟum. kā : kuṭṭaṉ = āṭṭukkuṭṭi. kuṭṭāṉ = ciṟupeṭṭi, ciṟu paṭappu. am + kuṭṭaṉ = aṅkuṭṭaṉ. itu vaṭamoḻiyil aṅkuṣṭaṉ eṉappaṭum.
akattiyar peyarāṉa aṅkuṭṭaṉ eṉpataiyum, peru viraliṉ peyarāṉa aṅkuṭṭaṉ eṉpataiyum, oṉṟāy mayakki, akattiyar aṅkuṭṭa (peruviral) aḷaviṉar eṉṟu paḻaimaiyar kūṟa nērntirukkalām.
(5) mānilattaic camaṉākkiyatu
paṉimalai orukālattil nīrkkīḻiruntu piṉpu veḷit tōṉṟiyatu. akattiyar teṟkē vantapiṉ, kumarināṭṭaik kaṭalkoṇṭatu. muṟkālattil muṟaiyē tāḻntum uyarntu mirunta vaṭateṉnilappākaṅkaḷ, piṟkālattil uyarntum tāḻntum pōyiṉa. iv viraṇṭaiyum iṇaittu, akattiyar tāḻntum uyarntumirunta vaṭateṉ nilappākaṅkaḷaic camappaṭuttiṉareṉṟum, vaṭapākam amiḻntataṟkuk kāraṇam nilattiṟkup pāramākumāṟu patiṉeṇ kaṇattavar vantu 
kūṭiya civaperumāṉ kaliyāṇameṉṟum kataikaṭṭiṉar paḻaimaiyar.
(6) tamiḻai uṇṭākkiṉatu
taÄœ mikap paḻaimaiyāṉatātalālum, piṟkālat tamiḻarkkuc carittiravaṟiviṉmaiyālum. itupōtu aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟa tamiḻ nūlkaḷuḷ akattiyam muṉṉatātalālum, akattiyar talaikkaḻakattiṟutiyil vantavarātalālum akattiyar tamiḻai uṇṭākkiṉar eṉṟu cilar karutalāyiṉar.
akattiyam vaḻinūlātal
akattiyam tamiḻilakkaṇa mutaṉūleṉṟu, ituvaraiyum kūṟappaṭṭuvantatu. iṟṟaiyārāycciyāl atu vaḻinūlē yeṉpatu teḷḷatteḷiyat terikiṉṟatu.
akattiyam vaḻinūl eṉpataṟkuk kāraṇaṅkaḷ
(1) tolkāppiyattil ōriṭattum akattiyam kūṟavum cuṭṭavum paṭāmai.
(2) eṉpa, eṉmaṉār pulavar, eṉa moḻipa, eṉṟiciṉōrē eṉṟu tolkāppiyar muṉṉūlāciriyaraip pallōrākak kuṟittal.
(3)	kaḷaviṉuṅ kaṟpiṉuṅ kalakka millāt
	 talaivaṉun talaiviyum pirinta kālaik
	 kaiyaṟu tuyaramoṭu kāṭcik kavāvi
	 evvamoṭu puṇarntu naṉimikap pulampap
	 pāṭap paṭuvōṉ patiyoṭu nāṭṭoṭu
	 muḷḷuṟut tiṟiṉē yuyarkaḻi yāṉantap
	 paiyu ḷeṉṟu paḻittaṉar pulavar
eṉṟu akattiyar muṉṉōr moḻiporuḷai eṭuttōtutal. cilar āṉantak kuṟṟam piṟkālattut tōṉṟiyateṉṟu kūṟuvar. atu tavaṟākum.

 celvōr ceppiya mūtā ṉantamum	(puṟat. 19)
eṉṟu āṉantap peyar tolkāppiyattiṟ kūṟappaṭutal kāṇka.
ēḻiyaṉ muṟaiya tetirmuka vēṟṟumai
 vēṟeṉa viḷampāṉ peyaratu vikārameṉ
 ṟōtiya pulavaṉu muḷaṉoru vakaiyā
 ṉintira ṉeṭṭām vēṟṟumai yeṉṟaṉaṉ
eṉṟu akattiyar tamakku muntiṉa iru nūlāciriyarai vitantuṅ kūṟiṉar.
akattiyattiṟkumuṉ, intiraṉ eṉṟoru pulavaṉ iyaṟṟiya aintiram eṉṉum ilakkaṇam iruntamai,
malkunīr varaippiṉ aintira niṟainta
 tolkāp piyaṉeṉat taṉpeyar tōṟṟi
eṉṟu tolkāppiyap pāyirattiṟ kūṟiyatā laṟiyalām.
“ēḻiyaṉ muṟaiyatu eṉṉum akattiya nūṟpāvil kuṟikkappaṭṭa iru nūlkaḷaiyum muṟaiyē vaṭamoḻi ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷākiya pāṇiṉīyamum aintiramumeṉak koṇṭaṉar pallōr. iJ tamiḻiṉ toṉmaiyai aṟiyāmaiyāl nērnta tavaṟākum.
pāṇiṉi ki.mu. 5ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉarā yirukka, avariyaṟ ṟiya ilakkaṇam eṅṅaṉam irāmar kālattavarāṉa akattiyar nūlukku mutaṉūlātal cellum?
aintiram eṉpatu intiraṉ eṉṟōr (āriyap) pulavar iyaṟṟiya ilakkaṇamākum. akattiyarkku muṉṉamē orucila āriyar teṉṉāṭṭiṟku vantiruntamai, aṟuvakaip paṭṭa pārppaṉap pakkamum eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kūṟuvatālum, kantapurāṇattālum, tirumāliṉ mutalaintu tōṟṟaravuc ceytikaḷālu maṟiyappaṭum.
aintiram eṉpatu vaṭamoḻi yilakkaṇamā teṉmoḻi yilakkaṇamā eṉṟu tiṭṭamāyk kūṟa iyalavillai. āyiṉum, intiraṉ - aintiram eṉṉum tiripākupeyar (tattitānta) muṟaiyiṉālum, aintirattiṉum vēṟāka muntunūl kaṇṭu eṉac cila nūlkaḷaip paṉampāraṉār kuṟittalālum 
atu vaṭamoḻi yilakkaṇamē yeṉak koḷḷa iṭamuṇṭu.

eṉṟu cilappatikārattil, nāṭukāṇ kātaiyiṟ kūṟiṉatu meṉka.
aintiram niṟainta tolkāppiyaṉ eṉṟu tolkāp piyar ciṟappikkappaṭuvatāl, aintiram mika viḻumiya nūleṉpatu peṟappaṭum. intiraṉ marutanilattup paṇṭait tamiḻt teyvamātaliṉ, aintiram ataṉ ciṟappuppaṟṟiyum nūlāciriyaṉ peyaroppumaipaṟṟiyum at teyvattiṉatākak kūṟappaṭṭatu. iṟaiyaṉār akapporuḷ ataṉ nūlāciriyaṉ peya
roppumaipaṟṟiyum, kōyiṟ pīṭattaṭiyiṟ kiṭantamaipaṟṟiyum, civaperumā ṉiyaṟṟiyatākak karutappaṭṭamai kāṇka. intiraṉuk kup paṇṭait tamiḻppeyar vēntaṉ eṉpatu. intira teyvattiṟkup paṇṭait tamiḻnāṭṭilirunta perumaiyaic cilappatikārattāṉum maṇimēkalaiyāṉu muṇarka.
tamiḻil 8 vēṟṟumaiyum orē kālattil tōṉṟiṉavai yalla. mutalāvatu 5 allatu 6 vēṟṟumaikaḷtām tōṉṟi yirukka muṭiyum. iṟutiyil tōṉṟiṉatu eṭṭām vēṟṟumai. atu oru kālattil mutal vēṟṟumaiyiṉ vēṟupāṭeṉṟu ataṉuḷ aṭakkappaṭṭatu. itaiyē,
ēḻiyaṉ muṟaiya tetirmuka vēṟṟumai
 vēṟeṉa viḷampāṉ peyaratu vikārameṉ
 ṟōtiya pulavaṉu muḷaṉ
eṉpatu kuṟikkum. āṅkilattilum viḷivēṟṟumaiyai mutal vēṟṟumaiyiṉ vēṟupāṭu (Nominative of Address) eṉṟē kūṟuvar.
aintira ilakkaṇattil, viḷivēṟṟumai muṉṉūlkaḷiṟ pōla mutal vēṟṟumaiyil aṭakkappaṭātu, taṉi vēṟṟumai yākak kūṟappaṭṭatu. itaiyē,
intira ṉeṭṭām vēṟṟumai yeṉṟaṉaṉ eṉṟu kūṟiṉar akattiyar.
vēṟṟumaikaḷ tōṉṟiṉa vakaiyum muṟaiyum piṉṉark kūṟappaṭum.
(4) akattiyam muttamiḻilakkaṇamātal
oru moḻiyil, mutaṉmuta lilakkaṇam tōṉṟumpōtu, kūṭiya pakkam eḻuttu col yāppu eṉṟa mūṉṟukkē tōṉṟum. akattiyattil im mūṉṟuṭaṉ poruḷilakkaṇamum, ataṉ mēlum icai nāṭaka vilakkaṇaṅkaḷum kūṟappaṭṭiruttaliṉ, atu mutaṉūlāyirukka muṭiyāteṉpatu tēṟṟam.
akattiyattiṟku āriyavilakkaṇat toṭarpiṉmai
akattiyam vaṭamoḻi yilakkaṇa nūlkaṭku mikamika muntiyatātalāṉum, tamiḻilakkaṇattiluḷḷa kuṟiyīṭukaḷellām centamiḻc coṟkaḷē yātalāṉum, vaṭamoḻi yilakkaṇat tilillāta cila collamaitikaḷum poruḷilakkaṇamum icai nāṭakavilakkaṇamum tamiḻiluṇmaiyāṉum, āriyar intiyāviṟ kuḷ puku muṉpē tirāviṭar talaiciṟanta nākarikam aṭaintirunta maiyāṉum. akattiyattiṟkum vaṭamoḻi yilakkaṇaṅkaṭkum eṭṭuṇaiyum iyaipiṉ ṟeṉka.
āyuṅ kuṇattava lōkitaṉ pakka lakattiyaṉkēṭ
 ṭēyum puvaṉik kiyampiya taṇṭami ḻīṅkuraikka
eṉṟu puttamittiraṉ kūṟiyatu, tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ pauttattaic ciṟappittaṟkuk kūṟiya puṉainturaiyē yākum.
tamiḻilakkiyattiṉ uccanilaik kālam 
(The Augustan Age of Tamil Literature)
tamiḻilakkiyattiṉ uccanilaikkālam talaikkaḻakak kālamē yeṉpataṟkuk kāraṇaṅkaḷ:
(1) tamiḻp payiṟci varavarak kuṟaital
talaikkaḻakak kālattil, tamiḻ muttamiḻāyiruntu, piṉpu iṭaikkaḻakak kālattil vevvēṟāyp pirintatu. kaṭaikkaḻakak kālattil icai nāṭaka ülkaḷiruntumòlavarāṟbparumpālum,gyilappaṭavillai.ïJnghnjh, icai nāṭaka nūlkaḷ illāmaiyuṭaṉ, iyaṟṟamiḻum cariyāyk kaṟkappaṭavillai, iyaṟṟamiḻp pakutikaḷil mukkiyamāṉa poruḷilakkaṇam terinta pulavar itupōtu mikac cilarēyāvar.
(2) mukkaḻakaṅkaḷum muṟaiyē oṭuṅkal
mukkaḻakaṅkaḷum kālattiṉālaṉṟit taṉmaiyiṉālum 
talaiyiṭai kaṭaiyāyiṉamai, kīḻkkāṇum kuṟippāl viḷaṅkum.

(3) kalaikaḷ varavara maṟaital
talaikkaḻakak kālattil ettuṇaiyō kalaikaḷ tamiḻiliruntaṉa. avai ovvoṉṟāy maṟaintupōyiṉa. iṉṟum nūlvaḻakki lillāviṭiṉum ceyalvaḻakkiṟ pala kalaikaḷuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu ciṟpam, ciṟpak kalaikkuriya akkirapaṭṭiyal, palakai, muṉai, itaḻ, kuṭam, tāṭi, kāl, nākapantam, pōtikai, yāḷam, kūṭu, nāṇutal, matalai, pūmuṉai, koṭi, cālai, kumpam, pīṭam, maṇṭapam, kōpuram, koṭuṅkai, curuḷyāḷi, tūṇ, paṭṭam, aḷavu, uttiram, muṭṭi pantam, koṭivaḷai, āḷāṅku, aṇiveṭṭikkāl, kōmukam mutaliya kuṟiyīṭukaḷ iṉṟum tamiḻāyuḷḷaṉa.1 piṟa kuṟiyīṭukaḷum taṟpōtu vaṭacollā yiruppiṉum, jmiḻiṉiṉṟumbmāḻipeyarkkapgṭṭavaiyē.ï‹W« tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ kaṭṭaṭat toḻil tamiḻarālēyē ceyyappaṭuvatum pārppaṉarāṟ ceyyappaṭā maiyuṅ kāṇka.
(4) ilakkaṇam varavarap piṟaḻtal 
tolkāppiyar kālattilēlē nūlil ilakkaṇap piḻaikaḷ tōṉṟiṉamai muṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭatu. atōṭu vaḻakkilum pala vaḻūu muṭipukaḷ piṟkālattil tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
alla eṉṉum paṭarkkaip palaviṉpāl etirmaṟaik kuṟippu viṉaimuṟṟē, ēṉai eṇṇiṭaṅkaṭkum vaḻaṅki varutal kāṇka.
(5) coṟkaḷ varavarap poruḷiḻattal
talaikkaḻakattiṟkup piṉ, oruporuṭ palacoṟkaḷaip pulavarkaḷ perumpālum tiṭṭamillātu vaḻaṅki vantiruk kiṉṟaṉar.
ī, tā, koṭu eṉpaṉa, muṟaiyē iḻintōṉ ottōṉ uyarntōṉ coṟkaḷākum. ivai piṟkālattil vēṟupāṭiṉṟi vaḻaṅkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
(6) tamiḻ varavarat tūymaikeṭal
tolkāppiyam muḻumaiyilum, aintāṟē vaṭacoṟkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. piṟkālattil avai varavarap perukiṉamaiyai, muṟaiyē, kaṭaikkaḻaka nūlkaḷ, tiruvācakam, kamparāmāyaṇam, villiputtūrāḻvār pāratam mutaliyavaṟṟai nōkkik kāṇka.
1	Dravidian Architecture,pp. 10-23
piṟkālattiṟ ceyyuḷ ceytār tamiḻiṉ tūymaiyaic ciṟituṅ karutāmal, mōṉaiyetukaiyoṉṟē nōkki, vaṭacoṟkaḷai ērāḷamāyum tārāḷamāyum vaḻaṅkiviṭṭaṉar.
(7) nūṟceyyuḷ varavara iḻital
mukkaḻakak kālaṅkaḷilum, pākkaḷālēyē perumpālum nūlka ḷiyaṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. piṟkālattār perumpālum pāviṉaṅ kaḷaiyē terintukoṇṭaṉar, pāviṉum pāviṉam iyaṟṟutaṟ keḷitāyiruttaliṉ.
(8) poruḷiṉum collē varavarac ciṟattal
kaḻakak kālaṅkaḷil colliṉum poruḷē ciṟanta teṉṟu, centoṭaiyāyiṉum poruḷ ciṟappac ceyyuḷ ceytaṉar. piṟkālattil, maṭakku, tiripu mutaliya collaṇi kaḷaiyum, palavakai ōviya(cittira)c ceyyuḷkaḷaiyum ciṟappākak koṇṭu, avaṟṟilēyē tam tiṟamaiyaik kāṭṭiṉar. poruḷaṇiyil iyaṟkaik karuttum collaṇiyil ceyaṟkaik karuttum amaital, ceyyuḷiyaṟṟip payiṉṟavar yāvarkkum pulaṉām.
(9) nūṟporuḷ varavara iḻital
muṟkālap pulavar mēṉāṭṭārpōlap palvakaik kalai nūlkaḷai iyaṟṟiṉar; piṟkālap pulavarō kōvai, ulā, antāti,  kalampakam mutaliya pukaḻnūl vakaikaḷaiyē iyaṟṟuvā
rāyiṉar. oruvar kōvai pāṭiviṭṭāl, talaiciṟanta pulavarāka matikkappaṭuvar. yāvaiyum pāṭik kōvaiyaip pāṭu eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
piṟkālattil vakutta 96 paṉuvalkaḷum perumpālum pukaḻ nūlvakaikaḷē. itaṉāl piṟkālattāratu kalaiyuṇarcciyiṉmai veḷiyākum.
ceyyuḷkaṭkum nūlaḷavukkum kulamuṟai vakuttatum, piṟkālattār pullaṟivāṇmaiyaik kāṭṭum.
kaṭaikkaḻakak kālattil nālvar taṉittaṉi nāṟpatu ceyyuḷkoṇṭa nūloṉṟai yiyaṟṟiṉar. piṟkālattup pāṭṭiyalkaḷ avaṟṟiṉ tokaiyiliruntum, nāṉāṟpatu eṉṟoru nūlvakaiyai vakuttukkoṇṭatu nakaippiṟ kiṭamāṉatē. kalaiyuṇarcci yiḻantamaiyālum, vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉ ṟum vanta paḻamaik kalviyiṉālum, carittiram tiṇainūl eṉṉum īr aṟiviyaṟkalaikaṭkum māṟākap pāviyaṅ(kāviyam)kaḷil, nāṭṭup paṭalam nakarap paṭalam mutaliya pakutikaḷil, tam mati nuṭpattaik kāṭṭuvatoṉṟē kuṟikkōḷākak koṇṭu, poyyum pulaiyumāṉavaṟṟaiyellām puṉaivārāyiṉar piṟkālap pulavar.
(10) āṭpeyarkaḷ varavara vaṭacollātal
kaṭaikkaḻakattiṟku muṉkālattil, aracar peyarkaḷ kaṭuṅkōṉ kāyciṉaṉ eṉac centamiḻp peyarkaḷā yirun tamaiyum, piṉkālattil jaṭilavarmaṉ parākkiramaṉ eṉa vaṭamoḻip peyarkaḷāṉamaiyum, paṭaittalaivaṉ, mēlōṉ eṉṉum taṉittamiḻp peyarkaḷiruppavum, avaṟṟukkup patilāy, muṟaiyē ēṉāti (cēṉāpati), eṭṭi (cirēṣṭaṉ) eṉṉum vaṭamoḻip peyarkaḷ paṭṭappeyarkaḷāy vaḻaṅkiṉamaiyuṅ kāṇka.
(11) varavara vaṭanūl tamiḻnūluk kaḷavaiyātal
(12) tamiḻar palatuṟaikaḷilum varavarak keṭṭuvarutal
kaṭaikkaḻaka ilakkiyam ōr ilakkiyamākāmai
palar kaṭaikkaḻaka ilakkiyamē tamiḻilakkiyam eṉṟu niṉaittukkoṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. atuvē ilakkiyamāyiṉ, tamiḻ puṉmoḻikaḷil oṉṟākavē vaitteṇṇappaṭum.
patiṉeṇ mēṟkaṇakkāṉa pattuppāṭṭu eṭṭuttokai eṉpavaṟṟuḷ, pattuppāṭṭu, aiṅkuṟunūṟu, patiṟṟuppattu, kalittokai eṉṟa nāṉkē nūl eṉṉum peyarkkuc ciṟitu urimaiyuṭaiyavai. ivaiyum pukaḻum akapporuḷum paṟṟiyavaiyē ēṉaiya nāṉkum taṉippāṭaṟ ṟiraṭṭukaḷ. patiṉeṇ kīḻkkaṇakku nūlkaḷ taṉinūlka ḷāyiṉum, naṉṉeṟi akapporuḷ eṉṉum iru poruḷ paṟṟiyaṉavē.
kaṭaikkaḻaka nūlkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum muppattāṟa ṉuḷḷum oṉṟāvatu kalaipaṟṟiyataṉṟu, pāviyamumaṉṟu. avaṟṟuḷ ācārakkōvaiyō vaṭanūl moḻipeyarppāyum piṟappiluyarvu tāḻvu vakuppatāyum, eḷiya poruḷkaḷaik kūṟuvatāyumuḷḷatu.
kaṭaikkaḻaka nūlkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum muppattāṟum, ak kaḻakak kālattilēyē tōṉṟiyavaiyalla. avaṟṟuṭ cila ataṉ piṉṉarē iyaṟṟavum tokukkavumpaṭṭaṉa. tokai nūlkaḷel lām oruvarē orē kālattil tokuttavaiyumalla.
mēlum, nālaṭiyār, nāṉmaṇikkaṭikai mutaliya pala kīḻkkaṇakku nūlkaḷ, kaṭaikkaḻakap pulavarā liyaṟṟappaṭā maiyum, mēṟkaṇakku patiṉeṭṭiṟkoppak kīḻkkaṇakku patiṉeṭṭu vakukkappaṭṭamaiyum, naṟṟiṇai, kuṟuntokai, akanāṉūṟu, puṟanāṉūṟu eṉpaṉa nannāṉūṟu pāṭalkaḷāyt tokukkappaṭṭamaiyum nōkki uṇarntukoḷka. patiṉeṇ kīḻkkaṇakkuḷ patiṉeṭṭāvatu nūl, kainnilaiyeṉṟum iṉṉilaiyeṉṟum iruvēṟu koḷkai nilavuvataiyum nōkkuka.
ciṟanta nūlkaḷellām akattiyarkku muṉṉamē tamiḻil iyaṟṟappaṭṭiruntaṉa. piṟkālattār avaṟṟai ārāyntē vantaṉar eṉpatai, 
talaiccaṅkamiruntār.... tamiḻārāyntatu kaṭal koḷḷap
paṭṭa maturaiyeṉpa. iṭaiccaṅkamiruntār.... tamiḻārāyntatu kapāṭapuratteṉpa. kaṭaiccaṅkamiruntu tamiḻārāyntār.... caṅka miruntu tamiḻārāyntatu uttaramaturai yeṉpa eṉṟu mukkaḻaka varalāṟṟiṟ kūṟiyatiṉiṉṟum aṟintukoḷka.
akattiyarkku muṉṉarē taṉittamiḻar tamiḻ vaḷarttamai
akattiyarkku muṉ intiraṉ eṉṟa ōr āriyar, tamiḻilak kaṇam ceytamai muṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭatu. av vintiraṉukku muṉ āriyar ilakkaṇam perumpālum ceytirukka muṭiyātu.
talaiccaṅkamiruntār.... nālāyirattu nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpatiṟṟi yāṇṭu caṅkamiruntāreṉpa. avarkaḷaic caṅkamirīiyiṉār kāyciṉavaḻuti mutalākak kaṭuṅkōṉīṟāka eṇpattoṉpatiṉ mareṉpa eṉṟu, talaikkaḻakamirunta kālam 89 araca talaimuṟai yiṉatākac collappaṭutalāl, akattiyar avvaḷavu kāla miruntirukka muṭiyāteṉpatu muḻuttēṟṟam. ākavē, talaik kaḻakattiṉ iṟutik kālattil, allatu ataṉ piṉtāṉ, akattiyar tamiḻnāṭṭiṟku vantirukkamuṭiyum. avar teṉmaturai ceṉṟa cila āṇṭukaḷukkuḷ, kumarināṭṭiṉ teṉpakutiyaik kaṭal koṇṭatu. piṉpu avar ōyvupeṟṟup potiyamalaiyil tavamiruntu tam iṟutikkālattaik kaḻittār. pāṇṭiyaṉ kapāṭapurattaik kaṭṭi aṅku iṭaik kaḻakattai niṟuviṉāṉ. atil tolkāppiyar talaimai tāṅkiṉār. akattiyar akkālattil iruntaṉarēṉum, ak kaḻakattil uṟuppiṉarā yiruntatākat teriyavillai. iṭaikkaḻakattilum avar uṟuppiṉarāyiruntatāka mukkaḻaka varalāṟu kūṟuvatu, avar akkālattiliruntamai paṟṟiyēyaṉṟi vēṟaṉṟu. talaikkaḻakattai avar amaittār eṉṟa varalāṟu poruntāteṉpatu muṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭatu. ovvoru kaḻakamum pala talaimuṟaiyāka naṭaipeṟṟateṉṟum, avaṟṟuḷ iṟutit talaimuṟaiyilirunta pulavar tokaiyē varalāṟṟiṟ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟateṉṟum aṟintu koḷka.
akattiyar, tolkāppiyar kālattiliruntu kaṭaikkaḻakak kālattil kapila paraṇa nakkīrarum, aṇmaik kālattil ṭākṭar u.vē. cāminātaiyar avarkaḷum, rāvcāhip mu. irākavai yaṅkār avarkaḷum, rā. irākavaiyaṅkār avarkaḷumākap perumpālum pārppaṉarē tamiḻnūluk katikārikaḷā yiruntu varuvatu evarkkum terintatē.
kumarināṭu evvaḷavu paḻaimaiyāṉatō avvaḷavu paḻaimaiyāṉatu tamiḻ eṉpatai avar aṟivārāka. toṇṭu, num eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷiṉ iyalpu akattiyar kālattavarāṉa tolkāppiyarāl aṟiyappaṭāmaiyālum, ikkālattiṉiṉṟu paḻaṅkālam nōkkic cellaccellat tamiḻil vaṭacoṟkaḷ kuṟaintukoṇṭē pōvatiṉālum, akattiyattilum tol kāppiyattilum aintāṟu coṟkaḷē vaṭacoṟkaḷāyirutta talāṉum, akattiyar kālattiṟku muṉpē eṭṭuṇaiyum vaṭacoṟ kalavāta taṉittamiḻ vaḻaṅkiyateṉṟum atu tamiḻa rālēyē vaḻaṅkappaṭṭateṉṟum aṟintukoḷka.
pērakattiyam eṉṟu itupōtu vaḻaṅkum nūl piṟ kālattil vaṭamoḻippaṟṟuḷḷa oruvarāṟ ceyyappaṭṭatu. atu pērilmaṭṭum akattiyam eṉka.
akattiyar ciṟṟakattiyam pērakattiyam eṉa iruvēṟu nūlkaḷ iyaṟṟiyatākat teriyavillai. akattiyam vaḻakkaṟṟapiṉ, ataṉ nūṟpāt tiraṭṭukaḷil, ciṟiyatum periyatumāṉa iraṇṭē muṟaiyē ap peyarpeṟṟu vaḻaṅkiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
vaṭacollait tamiḻcceyyuṭkuriya coṟkaḷil oṉṟākavum (ecca.1) moḻipeyarppai vaḻinūl vakaikaḷuḷ oṉṟākavum (marapu. 94) tolkāppiyar kūṟiyatu porunta villai. avaṟṟuḷ, vaṭacol orōvoṉṟu (vīṇāka) varuvatu avar kālattil uḷḷatēyāyiṉum, moḻipeyarp piṟkut tamiḻil iṭamiruntatākat teriyavillai. āṉālum, tolkāppiyattil aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiyiruppatāl, atu tamiḻi ṉiṉṟum moḻipeyartta vaṭanūlkaḷaiyē mutaṉūlkaḷākak kāṭṭum āriya ēmāṟṟu, avarkku muṉṉamē toṭaṅkiṉataiyē uṇarttuvatākum. itu vēṇṭāta vaṭa coṟkaḷ tolkāppi yattil vaḻaṅkuvataṉālum uṇarappaṭum. akkālattil iraṇṭoru coṟkaḷē āriyar tamiḻiṟ pukuttamuṭiyum eṉpataiyum uyttuṇarka.
akattiyarkkumuṉ tamiḻ caṟṟut taḷarntiruntamai
akattiyar kālattiṟkumuṉ, tamiḻnūlkaḷ ciṟitupōtu kaṟkappaṭātiruntamai, piṉvaruṅ kāraṇaṅkaḷāl viḷaṅkum.
(1) akattiyar murukaṉai nōkkit tavaṅkiṭakka, at teyvam tōṉṟi, avarkkuc cila ōlaiccuvaṭikaḷaik kāṭṭiṟṟu eṉṟa katai.
(2) akattiyar tamiḻai uṇṭākkiṉār eṉṟa vaḻakku.
(3) tolkāppiyap pāyirattil muntunūl kaṇṭu eṉṟu kūṟiyiruttal.
akattiyarkku muṉpē, cila āriyar tamiḻnāṭṭiṟku vantiruntamai muṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭatu. avarvayappaṭṭē pāṇṭiyaṉ tamiḻaic ciṟitupōtu vaḷarkkātiruntirukka vēṇṭum. kaṭaikkaḻattiṟkuppiṉ, pāṇṭiyariruntum kaḻakam niṟuvāmaiyum, vaṇaṅkāmuṭimāṟaṉ poyyāmoḻip pulavar kaḻakam niṟuvac coṉṉamaikku iṇaṅkāmaiyum nōkkiyuṇarka.
tolkāppiyar akattiyarkkut teriyāta cila toṉṉūlkaḷaik kaṇṭamaipaṟṟiyē, tolkāppiyar eṉṟu avar peyar peṟavum, muntunūl kaṇṭu eṉṟu pāyirattiṟ kuṟikkavum nērntateṉka. itai, perumpulavar mīṉāṭcicuntaram piḷḷaiyavarkaḷ kālattil kaḻaka nūlkaḷ aṟiyappaṭātiruntatum, avarukkut teriyāta mutunūlkaḷai avar māṇākkarāṉa ṭākṭar u.vē. cāminātaiyaravarkaḷ kaṇṭatumāṉa nilaikku oppiṭalām.
tolkāppiyar akattiyarkkut teriyāta muntunūl kaṇṭamaipaṟṟiyē, tam nūlil akattiyattaippaṟṟi eṅkēṉum kuṟippiṭavillai yeṉka. tolkāppiyam akattiyattiṟkup pintiṉatātaliṉ, atiṉum ciṟappāy ilakkaṇa meḻutappaṭṭatu. akattiyam vaḻakkaṟṟamaikku ituvum oru kāraṇamākum. itaiyaṟiyātār piṟkālattil, tolkāppiyar akattiyattaic cāvittatāka oru katai kaṭṭiṉar.
tolkāppiyar akattiyarait tam āciriyarākavēṉum ōriṭattum kuṟikkāmaiyāl, av viruvarkkumirunta toṭarpu, vētanāyakam piḷḷaikkum mīṉāṭcicuntaram piḷḷaikku miruntatu pōṉṟatō eṉṟu aiyuṟak kiṭakkiṉṟatu.
akattiyar tamiḻnūṟ payiṟciyaip putuppittamai
ṭākṭar u.vē. cāminātaiyar avarkaḷ, eṅṅaṉam maṭaṅkaḷiṉum tamiḻppulavar vīṭukaḷiṉum payilappaṭātu kuvintu kiṭanta pala tamiḻ nūlkaḷait tiraṭṭi acciṭṭut tamiḻulakukkut tantārkaḷō, aṅṅaṉamē akattiyarum ciṟitupōḻtu payilappaṭātu kiṭanta tamiḻnūlkaḷait tiraṭ ṭiyārāyntu, tamiḻnūṟ kalviyaip putuppittāreṉka.
itaṉāl, talaikkaḻakam muṭintapiṉṉarē akattiyar teṉṉāṭṭukku vantāreṉpatum, ak kaḻakam niṉṟupōṉa maikkuc cila āriyarē kāraṇamā yiruntirukkalāmeṉpatum, iṅṅaṉam tamiḻait taḷarttōrum tamiḻai vaḷarttōrumāka irucārār tamiḻnāṭṭiṟku vanta āriyar eṉpatum nuṇittaṟiyap paṭum.
mēṉāṭṭil mutaṉmutal ilakkaṇam varaintavar piḷāṟṟō (Plato, B.C. 427) eṉṟum, avar peyarccollum viṉaiccollumē kaṇṭupiṭittār eṉṟum ataṉpiṉ avartam māṇākkarāṉa arisṟṟāṭṭil (Aristotle, B.C. 384) iṭaiccollum eccamuṅ kaṇṭupiṭittāreṉṟum, mēṉāṭ
ṭilakkaṇaṅkaṭkellām aṭippaṭaiyāṉatum viḷakkamāṉatum uṇmaiyil ilakkaṇameṉṟu collattakkatum, ṭaiyōṉi ciyas tirāks (Dionysius Thrax, B.C. 100) eḻutiya ilakkaṇamē yeṉṟum mākkacu mullar kūṟukiṟār.
vaṭamoḻiyil niṟaivāṉa ilakkaṇamākiya pāṇiṉīyam ki.mu. 5ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉatu. ataṟku muṉṉamē pala ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyi liruntaṉa. āyiṉum, avai yāvum vaṭamoḻi mutaṟ pāviyamāṉa vāṉmīki yirāmāyaṇattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭavaiyē. vaṭamoḻiyilakkaṇaṅkaḷ mutaṉmutal āriya maṟaikkē eḻuntaṉavēṉum, avai tolkāppiyattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭavai eṉpataṟku eḷḷaḷavum aiyamillai. avai āriyar tamiḻilakkaṇattai yaṟintapiṉṉarē iyaṟṟappaṭṭavaiyeṉpatu piṉṉar viḷakkappaṭum.
iṅṅaṉam, ulakattilēyē mutaṉmutal tiruntiyatum ilakkaṇameḻutappeṟṟatumāṉa tamiḻ, itupōtu oru puṉmoḻiyiṉum iḻivā yeṇṇappaṭuvataṟkuk kāraṇam, iṟṟait tamiḻariṉ aṟiyāmaiyum maṭimaiyumēyaṉṟi vēṟaṉṟu.
āriyar tam maṟainūlkaḷait tamiḻarkku neṭuṅkālam maṟaittuvaittatu, avaṟṟiṉ tāḻvu veḷiyākāmaip poruṭṭēyaṉṟi, avaṟṟiṉ tūymaiyaik kāttaṟ poruṭ ṭaṉṟu. tamiḻiluḷḷa kalainūlkaḷai moḻipeyarttum, avaṟṟai virivākkiyum, vaṭamoḻiyilakkiyattai mika vaḷarttukkoṇṭa piṉpuṅkūṭa, avar tam maṟainūlkaḷait tamiḻarkku nērē
yaṟivikkavē yillai. mēṉāṭṭārē mutaṉmutal avaṟṟaik kaṟṟut tam moḻikaḷiṟ peyarttut tamiḻarkkaṟivittaṉar. ippōtu uṇmai veḷiyākiviṭṭatēyeṉṟu, āriyar tam muṉṉōr ki.mu. 2500 āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ iyaṟṟiya eḷiya maṟaimoḻikaṭku, iv virupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkuriya viḻumiya karuttukaḷai yellām poruttiyuraikkiṉṟaṉar. itaṉ poruntāmai ārāycciyillārkkup pulaṉākātupōyiṉum, aḵtuḷḷārkkup pōkāteṉka.
vaṭaticai uyarntum teṉticai tāḻntatum
akattiyar teṟkē vantapiṉ, nilam vaṭaticaiyil uyarntatum teṉticaiyil tāḻntatum muṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭatu. iṅṅaṉamē, kalvi, kaittoḻil, vāṇikam, celvam, aluval, atikāram mutaliya piṟavaṟṟilum avviru ticaikaḷum (āriyamum tirāviṭamum) muṟaiyē uyarntum tāḻntum pōyiṉaveṉ ṟaṟintukoḷka.
mēṟku kiḻakku eṉṉum ticaip peyarkaḷ pōṉṟē, uttaram (vaṭakku) takkaṇam (teṟku) eṉṉum ticaip peyarkaḷum ēṟṟa viṟakkaṅkaḷai yuṇarttuvatu piṉṉark kūṟappaṭum. 
kaṉṉaṭattil ilakkiyam tōṉṟiyatu ki.pi. 8ām nūṟṟāṇṭeṉṟum, teluṅkil tōṉṟiyatu ki.pi. 10ām nūṟṟāṇṭeṉṟum, malaiyāḷattil tōṉṟiyatu ki.pi. 13ām nūṟṟāṇṭeṉṟum collappaṭukiṉṟatu. tamiḻilakkiyam tōṉṟiya kālam iṉṉa nūṟṟāṇṭeṉṟu varaiyaṟuttuk kūṟa iyalātavāṟu, attuṇaip paḻaimaiyāyiruttaliṉ, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil tamiḻē moḻiyāyiṉum nūlāyiṉum - mikat toṉmai vāyntateṉpataic colla vēṇṭuvatēyillai.
tamiḻiṉ tiruttam
tirāviṭa moḻikaṭkuḷ tamiḻ mikat tiruntiyateṉpatu, ataṉ col vaṭivaṅkaḷālum ilakkaṇac ciṟappālum aṟiyappaṭum.
tamiḻccol vaḷam
(1) tamiḻiṉ vaḷam
tamiḻiṉ colvaḷattait tamiḻarē iṉṉum cariyāy uṇarntilar.
uṇmaiyuṭaimai yeṉṉuṅ kuṇam, niṉaivu col ceyal eṉṟa mukkaraṇaṅkaḷaiyun taḻuviyatu eṉpatai uṇarttaṟku, uṇmai (uḷ+mai), vāymai (vāy+mai), meymmai (mey+mai) eṉa mūṉṟucoṟkaḷ tamiḻiluḷḷaṉa.
maṉattiṉ vevvēṟu viṉaiyaiyuṅ kuṟittaṟku vev vēṟu col uḷatu.
kā : uḷ(ḷu), to will (uḷḷam - will); uṇar, to feel, to 
comprehend (uṇarvu - sentiment, uṇarcci - feeling); niṉai, to think, to remember (niṉaivu - thought, niṉaippu - memory); niṉaivukūr, to remember, to commemorate; K‹(D), to propose, to think, to intend (muṉṉam  - intention, indication); muṉṉiṭu, to propose, to set before; muṉṉiṭṭu, having proposed, having set before, for the purpose of. oru kāriyattai muṉṉiṭṭu eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka. muṉṉīṭu = proposal; uṉ(ṉu), to imagine (uṉṉam - imagination); uṉṉi, to guess (uṉṉippu - guessing); eṇ(ṇu), to deliberate, to consider (eṇṇam - consideration); karutu, to conceive, to think (karuttu - concept, idea); aṟi, to know (aṟivu - knowledge); koḷ, to opine (kōḷ - opinion, koḷkai - doctrine); mati, to estimate, to regard (matippu - estimation, approximation, respect, mati - sense); tīrmāṉi, to determine, to resolve; ea, to appreciate (nayappu - appreciation); teruḷ, to perceive clearly; maruḷ, to be deluded; āy, to test, to examine, (āyvu - test); ārāy, to make a critical study, to investigate (ārāycci - research, critical study); cūḻ, to counsel, to deliberate, cūḻavai - council.
uḷattoḻilpaṟṟi iṉṉum pala coṟkaḷuḷa. ivaṟṟoṭu tuṇaiviṉai cērttukkoḷḷiṉ, ikkālattiya maṉattoḻil nuṭpavēṟupāṭupaṟṟiya karuttukaḷai yellām kuṟikkac coṟkaḷai yamaittukkoḷḷalām.
maṉaivikkuk kaṇavaṉōṭuḷḷa pulaviyiṉ mūṉṟu nilaikaḷaiyuṅ kuṟittaṟku, muṟaiyē ūṭal, pulavi, tuṉi eṉa mūṉṟu coṟkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
oru viṉaikkut takunta camaiyam, nalla karuttil cevvi eṉṟum tīya karuttil aṟṟam eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
tamiḻiṉ colvaḷattai naṉṟāyuṇartaṟku nilaittiṇaic coṟkaḷai nōkkavēṇṭum.
mutalāvatu uḷḷīṭuḷḷa nilaittiṇai yuyirikaḷai marameṉṟum, aḵtillātavaṟṟaip pulleṉṟum iruvakaiyāka vakuttaṉar muṉṉōr.
puṟakkā ḻaṉavē pulleṉa moḻipa	(marapu. 81)
akakkā ḻaṉavē marameṉa moḻipa	(marapu. 21)
eṉpaṉa tolkāppiyam.
nilaittiṇai yuṟuppukaḷil ovvoṉṟiṟkum ataṉataṉ nuṭpa vēṟupāṭṭiṟkēṟpa vevvēṟu peyaruḷatu.
ilaiyeṉṉum orēyuṟuppiṟku nālvēṟu coṟkaḷuḷa. mā vāḻai mutaliyavaṟṟiṉatu ilai yeṉṟum, nel kēḻvaraku mutaliyavaṟṟiṉatu tāḷ eṉṟum, karumpu, peruñcōḷam mutaliyavaṟṟiṉatu tōkaiyeṉṟum, teṉṉai paṉai mutaliyavaṟṟiṉatu ōlaiyeṉṟuṅ kūṟappaṭum.
pū mutalāvatu tōṉṟumpōtu arumpu eṉṟum, pērarumpāṉapōtu pōtu eṉṟum, malarntapiṉ malar eṉṟum, viḻuntapiṉ vī eṉṟum, vāṭiyapiṉ cemmal eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. pū eṉpatu potuppeyar.
arumpu eṉṉum oru nilaikkē, ataṉataṉ aḷavukkut takkapaṭi, arumpu, moṭṭu, mukiḻ, mukai, mokkuḷ eṉa vevvēṟu coṟkaḷuḷa.
pūkkaḷ kōṭṭuppū, koṭippū, nīrppū, nilappū eṉa nālvakaiyāka vakukkappaṭum. ivaṟṟuṭaṉ, ceṭippū eṉpa toṉṟum cērttukkoḷḷalām.
kāyiṉ vevvēṟu nilaikaḷ pūmpiñcu, tirukupiñcu, iḷampiñcu, piñcu, araikkāy, kāy, mukkāṟkāy, kaṉṉaṟkāy allatu paḻakkāy, kaṭukkāy allatu karukkāy eṉa vevvēṟu coṟkaḷāṟ kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
māmpiñcu vaṭu eṉṟum, palāppiñcu mūcu eṉṟum vitappittuk kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
paruvattiṟ kāyppatu paruvakkāy eṉṟum, paruvamallāta kālattiṟ kāyppatu vampakkāy eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
mutirntapiṉ kaṉivillātatu kāy eṉṟum, kaṭiṉamāṉatu neṟṟu eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
cevvaiyāyp paḻukkāta paḻaṅkaḷ, vevvēṟu kāraṇam paṟṟic civiyal, cūmpal, vempal, cottai eṉap palavakai yākak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ, cottaivakai coṇṭu, cottai, coṭṭai eṉap pala peyarāṟ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. cottaiyāṉa miḷakup paḻam allatu vaṟṟal coṇṭu eṉṉuñ collāl maṭṭum kuṟikkappaṭutal kāṇka.
paḻattiṉ tōlvakaikaṭku, ataṉataṉ vaṉmai meṉmaik kut takkapaṭi toli, tōl, tōṭu, ōṭu, ciraṭṭai eṉap pala 
peyarkaḷuḷa.
vitaivakaikku vittu, vitai, maṇi, muttu, koṭṭai eṉa vevvēṟu coṟkaḷuḷa.
iṅṅaṉam, ovvōr uṟuppiṟkum paṟpala coṟkaḷuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟaiyellām eṉatu centamiḻc coṟpiṟappiyal akaramutaliyiṟ kaṇṭukoḷka.
nelvakai maṭṭum nūṟṟukkaṇakkā yuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ campā eṉṉum oruvakaiyē 70 tiṟamāyuḷḷatu.
āṅkilam ikkālattil palvēṟu moḻikaḷiṉiṉṟum ērāḷamāṉa coṟkaḷaik kaṭaṉkoṇṭiruppatāl, ekkaruttai yum terivikkavalla moḻiyākap pukaḻappaṭukiṉṟatu. āṉāl, iv virupatām nūṟṟāṇṭilum, āṅkilattilum atupōṉṟa piṟa perumoḻikaḷilum illāta pala coṟkaḷ ki.mu. 3000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻiliruntatu mikamika viyakkattakkatē.
tamiḻil oruporuṭ coṟkaḷ palaporuḷkaṭkuḷḷaṉa. piṟa tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ, pala poruḷkaṭkut tamiḻccoṟkaḷaiyē ovvoṉṟum ovvoṉṟākat terintukoṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.
kā : il - illu (teluṅku). maṉai - maṉa (kaṉṉaṭam).
(2) tamiḻttāymai
tirāviṭac coṟkaṭku mūlam perumpālum tamiḻilēyē uḷḷatu.
kā : veṉṉa (te.) < veṇṇey = veḷ + ney.
	     lētu (te.) < ilatu.
cey (kai) eṉpatu pōṉṟa iraṇṭoru coṟkaḷ, mūlavaṭivil teluṅkiliruppatu, potuvitikkut tavircciyē yaṉṟi māṟākāteṉka.
(3) tamiḻttūymai
tamiḻaip pōlap piṟa tirāviṭa moḻikaḷaip piṟa moḻik kalappiṉṟi yeḻuta muṭiyātu.
teluṅkil uṭalukku vaḻaṅkum oḷḷu, tēkam eṉṉum irucoṟkaḷil, oḷḷu eṉpatu uṭal eṉṉum tamiḻccolliṉ citaivu; tēkam eṉpatu vaṭacol.
(4) tamiḻiṉ muṉmai
piṟa tirāviṭa moḻiccoṟkaḷ mūlattiṟkut tamiḻaic cārntiruppatupōla, tamiḻccoṟkaḷ maṟittum tam mūlattiṟkup piṟamoḻiyaic cārntiruppavaiyalla.
(5) tamiḻal tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ ticaivaḻakkuttaṉmai
tamiḻallāta tirāviṭa moḻikaḷellām, āriyar intiyāviṟku varumuṉ, tamiḻiṉ kiḷaivaḻakkukaḷākavē yiruntaṉa.
tamiḻiluḷḷa coṟkaḷ, iyaṟcol tiricol ticaic col vaṭacol eṉṟu nālvakaiyākat tolkāppiyattil vakukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, mutal mūṉṟum taṉcol; iṟuti yoṉṟum ayaṟcol.
mutal mūṉṟaṉuḷ, muṉṉiraṇṭum centamiḻccol; piṉṉoṉṟum koṭuntamiḻccol.
centamiḻnāṭu
centamiḻ nilattai vaikaiyāṟṟiṉ vaṭakkum marutayāṟ ṟiṉ teṟkum karuvūriṉ kiḻakkum maruvūriṉ mēṟkumām eṉṟu iḷampūraṇar, cēṉāvaraiyar, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar mutaliyōr uraittaṉar .
centamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ ciṟanta pakuti iṉṟum pāṇṭi nāṭēyā yiruttaliṉ, iv vurai poruntātu.
centamiḻ nāṭāvatu: vaiyaiyāṟṟiṉ vaṭakkum, maruta yāṟṟiṉ teṟkum, karuvūriṉ kiḻakkum, maruvūriṉ mēṟkum eṉpa. ivvāṟu uraittaṟku ōr ilakkaṇaṅ kāṇāmaiyāṉum, vaiyai yāṟṟiṉ teṟkākiya koṟkaiyum, karuvūriṉ mēṟkākiya koṭuṅkōḷūrum, marutayāṟṟiṉ vaṭakkākiya kāñciyum tamiḻtiri nilamātal vēṇṭumātalāṉum, aḵtu uraiyaṉṟu eṉpār uraikkumāṟu :
vaṭavēṅkaṭan teṉkumari
 āyiṭait
 tamiḻkūṟu nallulakattu 
 vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷu māyiru mutaliṉ
 eḻuttuñ collum poruḷum nāṭi
eṉṟamaiyāṉum, itaṉuḷ tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakameṉa vicēṭit tamaiyāṉum, kiḻakkum mēṟkum ellai kūṟātu teṟkellai kūṟiyavataṉāṟ kumariyiṉ teṟkākiya nāṭukaḷaiyoḻittu, vēṅkaṭamalaiyiṉ teṟkum, kumariyiṉ vaṭakkum, kuṇakaṭaliṉ mēṟkum, kuṭakaṭaliṉ kiḻakkumākiya nilam centamiḻ nilameṉ ṟuraippa eṉṟuraittār teyvac cilaiyār.
iv vuraiyē ciṟantatākum. centamiḻ nilam,
cantaṉap potiyac centamiḻ muṉiyuñ
 cavuntara pāṇṭiya ṉeṉuntamiḻ nāṭaṉuñ
 caṅkap pulavarum taḻaittiṉi tirukkum
 maṅkalap pāṇṭi vaḷanā ṭeṉpa
eṉpatu piṟkālattiṟ kēṟṟatākum.
centamiḻ cērnta paṉṉiru nilattum
 taṅkuṟip piṉavē ticaiccoṟ kiḷavi	(tol. ecca. 4)
eṉpatāl, centamiḻnāṭ ṭellai tāṇṭiya paṉṉiru nāṭukaḷ koṭuntamiḻnāṭu eṉpatum, avaṟṟuḷ vitappāy vaḻaṅkiya 
coṟkaḷ ticaiccoṟkaḷ eṉpatum peṟappaṭum.
koṭuntamiḻnāṭu
koṭuntamiḻnāṭu paṉṉiraṇṭaiyum,
teṉpāṇṭi kuṭṭaṅ kuṭaṅkaṟkā vēṇpūḻi
 paṉṟi yaruvā vataṉvaṭakku - naṉṟāya
 cīta malāṭu puṉaṉāṭu centamiḻcēr
 ētamil paṉṉirunāṭ ṭeṇ
eṉṉum veṇpāviṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉavākak kūṟuvar pala uraiyāciriyar. ivaiyellām mutaṟkālattil centamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ pakutikaḷākak koḷḷappaṭṭamaiyiṉ, iv vurai poruntātu. in nāṭukaḷuḷ vēṇāṭu puṉaṉāṭu eṉṟa iraṇṭiṟkup patilāka, poṅkarnāṭu oḷināṭu eṉpavaṟṟaik kuṟippar iḷampūraṇar, cēṉāvaraiyar mutaliyōr. poṅkar nāṭṭaip potuṅkar nāṭeṉpar iḷampūraṇar. 
tāyait taḷḷai eṉpa kuṭanāṭṭār. nāyai ñamali eṉpa pūḻināṭṭār eṉṟu ticaiccoṟkuk kāṭṭuk kūṟiṉar iḷampūraṇar. iṅkuk kuṟikkappaṭṭa irunāṭukaḷum ippōtu malaiyāḷa nāṭṭiṉ pakutikaḷāka vuḷḷaṉa.
nacciṉārkkiṉiyar piṉvarumāṟu ticaiccoṟkuk kāṭṭuk kūṟiṉar:
teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭār ā erumai eṉpaṉavaṟṟaip peṟṟam eṉṟum; kuṭanāṭṭār tāyait taḷḷai eṉṟum, nāyai ñeḷḷai eṉṟum; kuṭṭanāṭṭār tantaiyai accaṉ eṉṟum; kaṟkānāṭṭār vañcaraik kaiyar eṉṟum; cītanāṭṭār ēṭāveṉpataṉai eluvaṉ eṉṟum, tōḻiyai ikuḷai eṉṟum, tammāmiyeṉpataṉait tantuvai eṉṟum; pūḻināṭṭār nāyai ñamali eṉṟum, ciṟukuḷattaip pāḻi eṉṟum; aruvānāṭṭār ceyyaic ceṟu eṉṟum, ciṟukuḷattaik kēṇi eṉṟum; aruvā vaṭatalaiyār kuṟuṇiyaik kuṭṭai eṉṟum vaḻaṅkupa.
iṉic ciṅkaḷam antōveṉpatu; karunaṭaṅ karaiya cikka kuḷira eṉpaṉa; vaṭuku ceppeṉpatu; teluṅku eruttaip pāṇṭileṉpatu; tuḷu māmarattaik kokkeṉpatu. oḻintavaṟṟiṟkum vantuḻik kāṇka.
itaṉāl teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam mutaliyavai orukālattil tamiḻiṉ ticaivaḻakkukaḷākavē yiruntaṉa veṉṟum, piṉpu kiḷai vaḻakkukaḷāki, iṟutiyil vaṭacoṟ kalappāl vēṟu moḻikaḷāyp pirintuviṭṭaṉa veṉṟum aṟiyappaṭum.
teyvaccilaiyārum ik karuttai yoṭṭiyē,
centamiḻ nāṭṭaic cērnta paṉṉiru nilattu muḷḷōr tattaṅ kuṟippiṉaiyuṭaiya ticaic collākiya col eṉṟavāṟu.
paṉṉiru nilamāvaṉa : vaiyaiyāṟṟiṉ... nāṭu, oḷināṭu, teṉpāṇṭināṭu, karuṅkuṭṭanāṭu, kuṭanāṭu, paṉṟināṭu, kaṟkānāṭu, cītanāṭu, pūḻināṭu, malāṭu, aruvānāṭu, aruvā vaṭatalai eṉpa. ivai centamiḻ nāṭṭakatta. centamiḻ (cērnta) nāṭeṉṟamaiyāl, piṟanāṭākal vēṇṭumeṉpār eṭuttuk kāṭṭumāṟu : kaṉṉit teṉkaraik kaṭaṟ paḻantīpam kollam kūpakam ciṅkaḷa meṉṉum ellaiyiṉ puṟattavum, kaṉṉaṭam vaṭukam kaliṅkam teliṅkam koṅkaṇam tuḷuvam kuṭakam kuṉṟakam eṉpaṉa kuṭapāliru puṟaccaiyattuṭa ṉuṟaipukūṟun tamiḻtiri nilaṅkaḷum, muṭi yuṭaiyavariṭu nilavāṭciyiṉ aracu mēmpaṭṭa kuṟunilak kuṭumikaḷ patiṉmarum uṭaṉiruppiruvaruvākiya paṉṉiruvar aracarum paṭaitta paṉṉiru tēyattiṉum, tamiḻc collātaṟku viruppuṭaiyaṉa eṉṟamaiyāṉum, tamiḻkūṟum nallulakattu...... poruḷum nāṭi eṉa niṟuttup piṉṉuñ centamiḻiyaṟkai civaṇiya nilattoṭu muntunūl kaṇṭu eṉa ōtiyavataṉāṟ civaṇiya nilamāvatu ellai kuṟitta nilattaic cārnta nilameṉa vēṇṭutalāṉum, paṉṉiru nilamāvaṉa :
kumariyāṟṟiṉ teṉkaraippaṭṭa paḻantīpamum kollamum kūpakamum ciṅkaḷamum, caiyattiṉ mēṟkuppaṭṭa koṅkaṇamum tuḷuvamum kuṭakamum kuṉṟakamum, kiḻakkuppaṭṭa karunaṭamum vaṭukum teliṅkum kaliṅkamum eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum.
ivaṟṟuḷ, kūpakamum kollamum kaṭalkoḷḷap paṭutaliṉ, kumariyāṟṟiṉ vaṭakaraiyaik kollameṉak kuṭiyēṟiṉār pōlum. pañcattirāviṭameṉavum vaṭanāṭṭār uraippavākalāṉ, avaiyaintum vēṅkaṭattiṉ teṟkātaluṅ kūṭāmai yuṇarka.
annilattu vaḻaṅkuñ collākic ceñcolliṉ vēṟupaṭṭuc cāṉṟōr ceyyuḷakattu varuvaṉa nīkkappaṭā eṉṟavāṟu.
kuṭāvaṭiyuḷiyam eṉṟavaḻi, kuṭāvaṭi eṉpatu kuṭakattār piḷḷaikaṭku iṭṭa peyar. antō eṉpatu ciṅkaḷavar aiyō eṉpataṟ kiṭṭa peyar. yāṉ taṟkaraiya varutu eṉṟavaḻi karaital eṉpatu karunāṭar viḷipporuḷuṇarak kūṟuvatu. ceppu eṉpatu vaṭukar collutaṟkup peyarāka vaḻaṅkuvatu. pāṇṭil eṉpatu teluṅkar pacuviṟkum eruttiṟkum peyarāka vaḻaṅkuvatu. kokku eṉpatu tuḷuvar māvukkup peyarāka vaḻaṅkuvatu. piṟavum ivvāṟu varuvaṉa palavaṟṟaiyum vanta vaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka eṉṟu kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka.2
teluṅku kaṉṉaṭa mutaliyavai piṟkālattil piṟamoḻi kaḷāyp pirintupōṉapiṉpu, avaṟṟiṉ muṉṉai nilaiyai
yuṇarātu avai vaḻaṅkum nāṭukaḷaiyum ayaṉmoḻi nāṭu kaḷaiyuñ cērttuk koṭuntamiḻnāṭākak kūṟiṉar pavaṇantiyār,
centamiḻ nilañcēr paṉṉiru nilattiṉum
 oṉpatiṟ ṟiraṇṭiṉil tamiḻoḻi nilattiṉum
 taṅkuṟip piṉavē ticaiccoṟ kiḷavi
eṉṟu. tamiḻoḻinta patiṉeṇ nilaṅkaḷai,
ciṅkaḷañ cōṉakañ cāvakañ cīṉan tuḷukkuṭakaṅ
 koṅkaṇaṅ kaṉṉaṭaṅ kollan teluṅkaṅ kaliṅkamvaṅkaṅ
 kaṅka makataṅ kaṭāraṅ kavuṭaṅ kaṭuṅkucalan
 taṅkum pukaḻttamiḻ cūḻpati ṉēḻpuvi tāmivaiyē
eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷāṉaṟika.
teluṅku kaṉṉaṭam mutaliyavai tamiḻiṉiṉṟum pirintu pōṉapiṉpē, poṅkarnāṭu oḷināṭu mutaliya paḻañcentamiḻ 
2	tol. ecca. nū. 1, 4 urai.
nāṭṭup pakutikaḷ koṭuntamiḻnāṭākak kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. itaṉāl, muṉpu koṭuntamiḻ nāṭāyiruntavai pala piṉpu piṟamoḻi nāṭāy māṟiviṭṭaṉa eṉpatum, centamiḻ nāṭu varavarat teṟku nōkki oṭuṅkikkoṇṭē varukiṉṟa teṉpatum, itaṟkuk kāraṇam āriyak kalappum centamiḻt toṭarpiṉmaiyum eṉpatum aṟiyappaṭum.
ceppu eṉpatu, collutal eṉṉum poruḷilmaṭṭum ticaiccollākum. maṟṟappaṭi atu centamiḻc collē eṉpatu,
ceppum viṉāvum vaḻāal ōmpal	(kiḷavi. 13)
aḵtaṉ ṟeṉpa veṇpā yāppē	(cey. 78)
eṉpavaṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭum.
ceppu eṉpatu, teluṅkil collutalaiyum, tamiḻil viṭai collutalaiyuṅ kuṟittal kāṇka. atōṭu tamiḻilakkaṇan tōṉṟiya toṉmutu kālattilēyē. ceppu eṉpatu ōr ilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭāyamaintataiyum nōkkuka. (ceppal veṇpā vōcai.)
ī tā koṭu eṉṉum mūṉṟu centamiḻc coṟkaḷil, ī eṉpatu eṅṅaṉam taṉ nuṇporuḷai yiḻantu teluṅkil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatō, aṅṅaṉamē ceppu eṉpatum eṉka.
tolkāppiyar kālattil, tamiḻil vaḻaṅkiya ayaṉmoḻic col vaṭacol oṉṟē. ataṉālēyē atu taṉ peyarāl vaṭa col eṉappaṭṭatu. ataṉpaṭi ippōtu tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum ayaṉmoḻic coṟkaḷaiyellām, āṅkilac col, intus tāṉic col eṉa avvam moḻippeyarālēyē kūṟal vēṇṭum. tirāviṭamoḻic coṟkaḷ maṭṭum ticaiccollā kavē kūṟappaṭum.
tolkāppiyar kālattil tamiḻil vaḻaṅkiṉa vaṭacoṟ kaḷum, arukiya vaḻakkēyaṉṟip perukiya vaḻakkaṉṟu. av varukiya vaḻakkum vēṇṭāmaiyāyt tamiḻaik keṭuttaṟkeṉṟē pukuttappaṭṭatākum.
vaṭacoṟ kiḷavi vaṭaveḻut torīi
 eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta collā kummē
eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kūṟiyatu, vaṭacol mēṉmēluṅ kalantu tamiḻt tūymaiyaik keṭukkātavāṟu oruvāṟu taṭai ceytatē yaṉṟi, ikkālattuc cilar eṇṇuvatu pōla vaṭacollaiyum piṟacollaiyum tārāḷamāyc cērttukkoḷḷumāṟu viṭai tantataṉṟu.
(8) tirāviṭam eṉṉuñ coṉmūlam
paḻaiya kālattil nāṭṭup peyarkaḷum moḻippeyar kaḷum perumpālum am īṟu peṟṟut tamiḻil vaḻaṅkiṉa.
kā : īḻam, kaṭāram, cīṉam, yavaṉam.
tamiḻam - tramiḷa(m) - tramiṭa(m) - traviṭa(m) - trāviṭa(m) eṉṉum muṟaiyil, tamiḻam eṉṉum collē tirāviṭam eṉṟu tirintatākum.
tamiḻam eṉpatu tamiḷa - taviḷa - taviṭa eṉṟu pirākirutattil tirintapiṉpu, tamiḷa taviṭa eṉṉum vaṭivaṅkaḷ tramila, tiramiṭa, traviṭa eṉṟu vaṭamoḻiyil tirintatāka, paṇṭitar kiraiyarcaṉ (Dr. Grierson) kūṟuvar.3 eṅṅaṉamiruppiṉum, tamiḻam eṉṉum collē traviṭa eṉṟu tirintateṉpataṟku eṭṭuṇaiyum aiyamillai.
kālṭuvel aiyar itupaṟṟit talaikīḻākak kūṟiṉar. avar tavaṟṟaik kiraiyarcaṉuṅ kuṟittuḷḷār.
tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ ellāvaṟṟiṟkum, tamiḻam eṉṉum peyarē potuppeyarāka mutalāvatu vaḻaṅki vantatu. trāviṭam eṉṉum vaṭivum, tamiḻam eṉṉum poruḷilēyē, mutaṉ mutal vaḻaṅkiyatākum. teluṅku tamiḻiṉiṉṟum pirintupōṉa piṉpu, muṉpu oṉṟāyeṇṇappaṭṭa tirāviṭa moḻi iraṇṭāyk karutappaṭṭu, āntira tirāviṭa pāṣā eṉṉum iṇaippeyarāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭatu. ataṉpiṉ, kaṉṉaṭam, malaiyāḷam mutaliya
3	Linguistic Survey of India, Vol. IV, p. 298
moḻikaḷ pirintu tirāviṭam palkiyapiṉ, tamiḻam eṉṉum peyariṉ maṟuvaṭivamāṉa tamiḻ tirāviṭam eṉṉum coṟkaḷuḷ, muṉṉatu tamiḻmoḻikkum piṉṉatu tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ ellāvaṟṟukkumāka varaiyaṟukkappaṭṭaṉa.
itukāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, tamiḻē tirāviṭa moḻikaṭkuḷ mikat toṉmaiyāṉatum mikat tiruntiyatum, mika vaḷamuḷḷatum, ataṉāl mikacciṟanta tirāviṭa eccamāṉatum eṉṟaṟintu koḷka.4
1. tirāviṭam vaṭakkunōkkit tirital
(i) tamiḻnāṭṭil, pāṇṭināṭṭup pakutiyākiya teṉpākamē iṉṟum tamiḻukkuc ciṟantatāka vuḷatu.
pāṇṭiyaṉukkut tamiḻnāṭaṉ eṉṟa peyar tivākarattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ākavē, pāṇṭināṭu tamiḻnāṭeṉac ciṟappikkappaṭṭatākum.
cōḻanāṭṭait tamiḻnāṭeṉṟum, toṇṭaināṭṭaic cāṉṟōruṭaittu eṉṟum kūṟiyatu piṟkālamākum. tamiḻaracark kuḷ, mutalāvatu āriyattiṟku muṟṟum aṭimaippaṭṭavaṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ. ataṉālēyē, iṉṟu teṉpākattil vaṭapākat tiliruppatupōṉṟa tamiḻuṇarcci yillai. āyiṉum, nāṭṭup puṟattiluḷḷa kuṭiyāṉavarkaḷiṭaip paṇṭait tamiḻccoṟ kaḷum vaḻakkukaḷum pala iṉṟum aḻiyātiruntu, pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ paḻam perumaiyai oru ciṟitu kāttukkoṇ ṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.
2. tamiḻnāṭṭil teṉpākam ciṟantateṉpataṟkuk kāraṇaṅkaḷ
(1)	tamiḻai vaḷartta allatu kātta mūṉṟu kaḻakaṅkaḷum pāṇṭināṭṭi liruntamai.
(2)	vaṭapākattil vaḻaṅkāta pala coṟkaḷum paḻa moḻikaḷum vaḻaṅkal.
(3)	vaṭacoṟkaḷ taṟpavamāka vaḻaṅkal.
	kā : cākṣi - cākki.

	cuttam, mirācutār mutaliya coṟkaḷukkut tuppuravu, paṇṇaiyār mutaliya taṉittamiḻc coṟkaḷ vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
(4)	coṟkaḷ uṇmai vaṭivil vaḻaṅkal.
	kā : irāmam - (nāmam).
(5)	ayalnāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vanta poruḷkaṭkut tamiḻppeyar vaḻaṅkal.
	kā : bicycle - mitivaṇṭi.
(6)	vaṭapākattilillāta pala payirkaḷ ceyyappaṭal.
	kā : kāṭaikkaṇṇi, cāmai, kutiraivāli.
(7)	ōviyavuṇarcci ciṟantiruttal.
(8)	callikkaṭṭu, cilampam mutaliya maṟaviḷaiyāṭṭukaḷ ciṟappāy vaḻaṅkal.

	kā : paracu (sweep) eṉṟa col vaḻaṅkāmai.

	paracu (pīrāy) mūñceli (mūñcūṟu).
 (iv)	ceṉṉaikku vaṭakkil moḻipeyartal.
vaṭavēṅkaṭam eṉṟu, vēṅkaṭam tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ vaṭavellaiyākat tolkāppiyap pāyirattiṟ kūṟap paṭṭuḷḷatu. āyiṉum, tamiḻkūṟum nallulakattu eṉṟataṉāl, ataic centamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ vaṭavellaiyākak koḷvatē poruttamākum.
vēṅkaṭam ippōtu teluṅka nāṭṭiṟku uṭpaṭṭu viṭṭatu.
teluṅku mikutiyum āriyattaṉmaiyaṭaintu, āriyattiṟkum tirāviṭattiṟkum iṭaippaṭṭa nilaiyiluḷḷatu.
teluṅkil eḻuttolikaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṟpōl vallōcai peṟukiṉṟaṉa; vakaram pakaramākavum ḻakaram ṭakaramākavum māṟukiṉṟaṉa; vaṭacoṟkaḷ mikat tārāḷamāy vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. oru ceyyuḷ perumpālum vaṭacoṟkaḷā yiruntāl, mikavuyarnta teluṅkāka matikkappaṭukiṉṟatu.
teluṅku vaṭivākap pala teṉcoṟkaḷ mēlaiyāriya moḻikaḷilum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.

kēḷ eṉṉum col, viṉavu eṉṉum poruḷil tamiḻil vaḻaṅkuvatupōla, aṭuku eṉṉum col teluṅkil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟa teṉka. Write eṉṉum collil (v) paṇṭu olittatu.
(v) teluṅkiṟku vaṭakkil āriyamoḻi vaḻaṅkal
paṇṭaik kālattil, tamiḻ, teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam, marāṭṭi, kujarātti eṉṉum aintaiyum, āriyarē pañca trāviṭī eṉṟu aḻaittaṉar. kālṭuvel aiyar ittokuppai oppuk koḷḷavillai. āyiṉum, ittokuppu cariyāṉatē yeṉpatu, immaṭalattiṉ 2ām pākattil viḷakkappaṭum.
(vi) vaṭa intiyāvil tirāviṭa maṟaivu
kōṇṭi (Gondi), patrī (Bhatri), mālṟṟō (Malto), pōy (Bhoi) mutaliya tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ, meḷḷa meḷḷa āriya mayamāvatai allatu maṟaintupōvataip paṇṭitar kiraiyarcaṉ 1906ām āṇṭē tamatu intiya moḻik kaṇakkīṭu eṉṉum nūliṟ kuṟittuḷḷār.5
5	L.S.I. Vol. IV, pp.446, 472-4
(vii) vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷil tirāviṭa aṭaiyāḷam
 āriyar varumuṉ vaṭa intiyāvil vaḻaṅkiya tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ, avar vantapiṉ āriyattoṭu kalantupōṉamai yāl, āriyattiṟku māṟāṉa pala tirāviṭa amaitikaḷ iṉṟum vaṭanāṭṭu moḻikaḷiluḷḷaṉa. avaiyāvaṉa:
(1)	pirikkappaṭum īṟṟurupāl vēṟṟumaiyuṇarttal.
(2)	īreṇṇiṟkum vēṟṟumaiyuru poṉṟāyiruttal.
(3)	muṉṉilaiyai uḷappaṭuttuvatum uḷappaṭuttātatu māṉa iru taṉmaippaṉmaip peyarkaḷ.
(4)	muṉṉoṭṭukkup patilākap piṉṉoṭṭu vaḻaṅkal.
(5)	viṉaiyeccattāṟ kālam amaital.
(6)	taḻuvuñ coṟṟoṭar taḻuvappaṭuñ coṟṟoṭarkku muṉṉiṟṟal.
(7)	taḻuvuñcol taḻuvappaṭuñcoṟku K‹Å‰wš.6
intiyil pala tamiḻccoṟkaḷum ilakkaṇa amaitikaḷum vaḻakkukaḷum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.

ilakkaṇa amaiti
(1) 4ām vēṟṟumaiyurupu tamiḻil ku eṉṟum intiyil kō eṉṟu mirukkiṉṟatu.
(2) iru moḻikaḷilum vēṟṟumaiyaṭiyuṭaṉ urupu cērntu mūviṭappeyarkaḷ vēṟṟumaippaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
kā : eṉmēl, mujmē
(3) tamiḻil iya eṉpatum, intiyil (muṉṉilaiyil) iyē eṉpatum viyaṅkōḷ īṟākavuḷḷaṉa.
(4) māṟē7 (māṟu + ē) eṉpatu irumoḻiyilum ētup poru ḷiṭaiccollā yuḷḷatu.
intiyil kē eṉṉum urupōṭu cērntē varum.
(5) intiyil, ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟāviṉaip pakutikaḷ āv (vā jāv pō) eṉṉum ēvalorumaiyuṭaṉ kūṭi, iṟatta kāla viṉaiyeccap poruḷpaṭum.
kā: suṉ jāv = kēṭṭuviṭṭuppō.
7	puṟam. 4, 20, 22, 92-3, 271, 380; naṟ. 231
iṅṅaṉam tamiḻilumuṇṭu. āṉāl, viṉaippakutikaḷ iṟantakāla viṉaiyeccap poruḷpaṭāmal nikaḻkāla viṉaiyeccap poruḷpaṭum.
kā : kēḷvā = kēṭkavā; kēḷpō = kēṭkappō.
(6) intiyil iṟantakāla viṉaiyeccaṅkaḷum muṟṟukaḷum āṇpālorumaiyil ākāra vīṟāyuḷḷaṉa.
kā : āyā = vantāṉ, vantu.
	     pōlā = coṉṉāṉ, colli.
ivai ceyyā eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉaiyeccamē paṇṭait tamiḻilum viṉaimuṟṟāy vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. pāluṇarttum īṟu piṟkālattiṟ cērkkappaṭṭatu.
vaḻakku : pallaip piṭuṅkiviṭu eṉṉum vaḻakku ceruk kaṭakku eṉṉum poruḷil, tānt kaṭṭēkartō eṉṟum, uyiraik kaiyilēntikkoṇṭu ōṭu eṉpatu jāṉlēkkar pāk eṉṟum intiyil vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka.
(viii) intiyāviṟku mēṟkē peluccistāṉa malaināṭṭil pirāhuyī eṉṉum tirāviṭac ciṟupuṉmoḻi vaḻaṅkal.
(ix) peluccistāṉattiṟkappāl tirāviṭamoḻi vaḻaṅkāmaiyum, tirāviṭac coṟkaḷē vaḻaṅkutalum.
oru moḻi taṉṉāṭṭiṟ cevvaiyāyiruntu, ayalnāṭu cellaccellat tirivatu iyalpu. āṅkilam iṅkilāntil cevvaiyākavum, intiyāvil tirintum, āppirikkā cīṉam mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiṟ citaintum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉamē tamiḻ allatu tirāviṭamoḻi teṉṉāṭṭiṟ cevvaiyāyum vaṭakkē cellaccellat tirintum vaḻaṅkuvatiṉāl, tirāviṭariṉ tollakam kumarināṭēyeṉpatu tuṇiyappaṭum.
3. tamiḻakattiṉ toṉmaik kuṟippukaḷ
(1) nilattiṉ toṉmai
ñālattil kumarināṭu mikat toṉmaiyāṉatu. intiyā viṉ mutaṟ peyar nāvalantīvu eṉpatu. itu nāvalan taṇ poḻil eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum. tamiḻnāṭu muḻuvatum orē poḻil (cōlai) pōliruntamaiyiṉ, poḻil eṉpatu nāṭu allatu ulakam eṉṟu poruḷpaṭṭatu. itaṉāl tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ makkaṭ perukkaṟṟa oru toṉmutunilai yuṇarappaṭum.
carittira kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa tāḻikaḷum aṭakkak kaṟkaḷum ēṉaṅkaḷum, teṉṉāṭṭiṟ palaviṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.
mikap paḻaimaiyāṉa maṇṭaiyōṭukaḷuḷ oṉṟu, jāvāviṉiṉṟu vantuḷḷatu. at tīvu taṉṉarukiluḷḷa piṟa tīvukaḷuṭaṉ orukālattil āciya nilattō ṭiṇaikkap paṭṭiruntatu. atu kāṭṭumāntaṉ (orang-utan) eṉṉum 
kuraṅku vatiyum iṭaṅkaṭku aṇittāṉatu. māntaṉukkum kuraṅkiṟkum iṭaippaṭṭa ōr uyir vatinta iṭamākat teritalāl, atu namakku mika mukkiyamāṉatu8 eṉṟu ālpvreṭ kiḷāṭ (Alfred Clodd) kūṟuvatāl, ataṉōṭu oru kālattil iṇaikkappaṭṭirunta kumarināṭṭiṉ toṉmaiyum oruvāṟu viḷaṅkum.
yāṉaikkaiyum maṭaṅkaluṭampum uḷḷa yāḷi eṉṉum vilaṅkum, atupōṉṟa piṟavum paṇṭait tamiḻnāṭṭiliruntatum ataṉ toṉmaiyaip pulappaṭuttum.
(2) nilattumoḻiyiṉ toṉmai
itu piṉṉark kūṟappaṭum
(3) nilattumakkaḷ vāḻkkaimuṟai
nāṭṭuvāḻkkainilai: ikkālattiṟpōlac carittira nūlkaḷ muṟkālattil eḻutappaṭāviṭiṉum, mutaṟṟamiḻariṉ vāḻkkai muṟai akapporuṭ ceyyuḷkaḷil, ciṟappākak kōvaiyil, mikak kāvalākap pōṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. makkaḷ evvaḷavu nākarikamaṭaiyiṉum, ataṉāl avaratu naṭaiyuṭai koḷkai evvaḷavu māṟiṉum, paṇṭaimuṟaipaṟṟiyē eṉṟum pāṭavēṇṭumeṉṟu kōvaikku oru marapuḷḷatu. atu 
8	Childhood of the World
pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkam eṉappaṭum. atāvatu, uḷḷatum illatuṅ kalantu iṉiyatākavē yirukkum nāṭaka muṟaiyum, iṉiyatum iṉṉātatuṅ kalantu uḷḷatākavē irukkum ulakiyal muṟaiyum oruṅkē taḻuviya nūṉeṟi vaḻakkākum.
kuṟiñcināṭṭaracaṉ makaḷukku uṭaiyum aṇiyum taḻaiyākavē kōvaiyiṟ kūṟiyiruppatu, mikap paḻaṅkālattu iyalpaik kuṟippatākum. ikkālattiṟ karāccip paṭṭaṇiyum oru peṇṇaikkuṟittuk kōvai pāṭiṉum, paṇṭait taḻaiyē talaivaṉ kaiyuṟaiyākak kūṟappaṭuvataṉṟik karāccippaṭṭu kūṟappaṭātu. iṅṅaṉamē piṟavum. 
paṇṭaittamiḻar kuṟiñci, mullai, marutam, neytal, pālai eṉa aintu vakaiyāṉa nilattiṟ kuṭiyiruntaṉar. ivai aintiṇai yeṉappaṭum. ivai nilaittiṇaiyāṟ peyar peṟṟaṉa (iṭavaṉāku peyar). ivaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷuḷ, pālai marutam eṉṉum iraṇṭum marappeyarkaḷ; ēṉaiya pūppeyarkaḷ.
pālai eṉpatu piṟanāṭṭiluḷḷatupōṉṟa vaṟaṇṭa maṇal nilamaṉṟu. kuṟiñcikkum mullaikkum iṭaiyiluḷḷa nilam pālaimarac ciṟappāl pālaiyeṉappaṭṭatu. atu mutuvēṉiṟ kālattil vaṟaṇṭum maṟṟak kālattil ceḻittumirukkum.
vēṉalaṅ kiḻavaṉoṭu veṅkatir vēntaṉ
 ṟāṉalan tirukat taṉmaiyiṟ kuṉṟi
 mullaiyuṅ kuṟiñciyu muṟaimaiyiṟ ṟirintu
 nalliyal piḻantu naṭuṅkutuya ruṟuttup
 pālai yeṉpatōr paṭivaṅ koḷḷum	(cilap. 11 : 12-16)
eṉṟu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ kūṟutal kāṇka. pālaiyiṉ mutuvēṉiṟkāla nilaiyē, piriviṟkuriyatāka akapporuṭ ceyyuḷkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭum.
makkaḷ aintiṇai nilattiṟkup pirintupōṉatu, kuṟiñci
yiṉiṉṟu pōvatum, ayalnāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vantu kuṭipukuntu pōvatumāka iruvakai. ivaṟṟuḷ, muṉṉatu makkaṭperukkāl paṭippaṭiyāy nikaḻvatu; piṉṉatu terintukōṭalāl orē camaiyattum nikaḻakkūṭiyatu. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉatē tamiḻnāṭṭil nikaḻntateṉka.
kuṟiñciyil maṭṭum makkaḷirunta kālamumuṇṭu. atu māntaṉ tōṟṟattiṟku aṭutta nilaiyākum. tamiḻnūlkaḷ tōṉṟiyatu marutattil nakaram tōṉṟiyapiṉpātaliṉ, kuṟiñciyilmaṭṭum makkaḷirunta toṉṉilai appōtu maṟaintupōyiṟṟu. ataṉāṟ kuṟikkappaṭavillai. āyiṉum ataik karuttaḷavaiyāṉ aṟintukoḷḷalām.
māntaṉ tōṟṟam āṇum peṇṇumāyttāṉiruntirukka vēṇṭum. avaraiyē ātam ēvaiyeṉṟu kiṟittumatamum islām matamuṅ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
irumutu peruṅkuravariṉiṉṟum pala makkaḷ tōṉṟiya piṉ, kuṟiñciyil iṭampōtāmal, cilar mullaikkuc ceṉṟaṉar.
mutaṟkālattil uṇavu tēṭuvatē māntar toḻilā yiruntatu. kuṟiñciyil kāykaṉikaḷaip paṟittum vēṭṭaiyāṭiyum uṇṭuvanta makkaḷ, iyaṟkaiyāy viḷaiyum maravuṇavu pōtāmaiyālum, vēṇṭiyapōtellām ūṉuṇavu kiṭaiyāmaiyālum, ceykaiyāyp payirpaccaikaḷaiyum vilaṅkukaḷaiyum vaḷarkkattoṭaṅkiṉar. itaṟku maramaṭarnta kuṟiñci vacatiyāyirāmaiyāl mullaikkuc ceṉṟaṉar. itaṉāl koṭiya vilaṅkukaṭkum ōraḷavu tappiṉar.
māntaṉ mutaṉmutalāy vaḷartta vilaṅku āvē. ā eṉpatu mā eṉpataṉ mey nīkkam. mā eṉṟu kattuvatu māveṉappaṭṭatu. mā-māṉ-māṭu. mā eṉpatu ṉakara meyyīṟu peṟṟu, āviṟkiṉamāṉa māṉai uṇarttiṟṟu. ā eṉpatu māṭu eṉpatupōla mutalāvatu potuppeyarā yiruntu, piṉpu peṇpālukku varaiyaṟukkappaṭṭatu. mutalāvatu vaḷarkkappaṭṭa vilaṅku mā(ā) vātaliṉ ataṉ peyar vilaṅkiṉattiṟkellām potuppeyarāyiṟṟu. ā eṉpatu ṉakara vīṟupeṟṟu āṉ eṉṟāyiṟṟu.
maravuṇavum ūṉuṇavum makkaṭku vēṇṭiyavāyuḷḷa maiyiṉ, uḻavum āvōmpalum oruṅkē tōṉṟiṉaveṉ ṉalām. āvāṉatu pāl tantatuṭaṉ uḻaviṟku vēṇṭiya kaṉṟukaḷaiyum īṉṟatu. pulveḷikaḷil māṭukaḷai mēyttuk koṇṭu, ciṟitu puṉceyp payirkaḷaiyum viḷaittuk koṇṭaṉar mullainilattār.
māṭu paṟaiyarālmaṭṭum tiṉṉappaṭukiṉṟatu. mēṉāṭu kaḷil atai ellārum uṇkiṉṟaṉar.
mullainilattiṟ pōtumāṉa nīrvaḷamum nilavaḷamumil lāmaiyāl, makkaḷ aṭuttāṟpōl, mīṉaip peritum virumpiṉavar neytalnilattiṟkum kūlattaip peritum viruppiṉavar marutanilattiṟkumākap pirintupōyiṉar.
pālainilavāṇarkku mutuvēṉilil uṇavu kiṭaiyāmai yāl, aṉṟu avar āṟalaikkavum cūṟai koḷḷavum nērntatu.
 aintu nilattilum māntar paraviyapiṉ, kuṟiñcinilattār kuṟi colvatāl kuṟavar eṉṟum, pālainilattār pōrt toḻil ceytu maṟam (vīram) ciṟantiruntamaiyiṉ maṟavar eṉṟum, mullainilattār kuṟiñcikkum marutattiṟkum iṭaiyilirun tamaiyāl iṭaiyar eṉṟum āvaik kāttalāl āyar eṉṟum, marutanilattār uḻavaic ciṟappāyc ceytatāl uḻavar eṉṟum, neytalnilattār paṭavut toḻil ceytamaiyiṉ paṭavar allatu paravar eṉṟuṅ kūṟappaṭṭār.
paṭaku paṭavu. ka-va, pōli. paṭavar-paravar-paratavar. ṭa - ra, pōli. paṭavar cenniṟamāyiruppatāl cempaṭavar eṉappaṭṭār. paratavar virittal tiripu.
nakaravāḻkkai nilai
māntar marutanilattiṟku vantapiṉ nilaiyāyk kuṭiyirukkat toṭaṅkiṉar; ataṉāl kuṭiyāṉavar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. nilaiyāyk kuṭiyiruntatiṉāl, marutanilattūrkaḷ mūtūrum pērūrum nakarum mānakaru māyiṉa. marutanilattūrkaḷ piṟa nilattūrkaḷiṉum palavakaiyiṟ ciṟantiruntamaiyāl, ūr eṉṟē yaḻaikkappaṭṭaṉa.
ūr nakar eṉṉum peyarkaḷ, mutalil taṉi vīṭṭaiyum, piṉpu, vīṭṭut tokutiyāṉa ūraiyum kuṟittaṉa. nakar eṉpatu piṟkālattil pērūrukku varaiyaṟukkappaṭṭatu.
nilaiyāṉa vāḻkkaiyālum, māntar perukkālum, mutaṉ mutal nakarattilēyē nākarikam ciṟappāyt tōṉṟiṟṟu. nākarikam eṉṉum collum nakaram eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟum piṟantatē. atu muṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭatu.
araciyal
māntar nilaiyāyuḷḷa iṭattil āṭporuṭ pātukāp piruppatum, nilaiyillāta iṭattil avaiyillātiruppatum iyalpu. marutanilattil māntar nilaiyāyiruntamaiyiṉ, āṭporuṭ pātukāppiṟku mutalāvatu kāvalum, piṉpu ūrāṇmai nāṭṭāṇmaikaḷum, ataṉ piṉ araciyalum tōṉṟiṉa.
kuṭikaḷai oru mantaipōṉṟum aracaṉai ataṉ mēyp paṉ pōṉṟuṅ karutiṉar. ataṉāl, aracaṉ kōṉ eṉappaṭ ṭāṉ. avaṉ kaiyil, araciṟku aṭaiyāḷamāka oru mēyppaṉ kōluṅ koṭukkappaṭṭatu. atu nērāyiruntamaipaṟṟic ceṅkōl eṉappaṭṭatu. ceṅkōl cemmaiyāṉa aracāṭcikku aṭaiyāḷam.
kō = pacu. kō + aṉ = kōvaṉ - kōṉ - kō(ṉ). kōṉ eṉṉum col āveṉṉum poruḷil āriya moḻikaḷiṟ peruvaḻakkāka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
Swed. - Dan. ko, Du. koe, Skt. go, Ger. kuh, Ice. kyr, Irish - Gael. bo, L. bos, Gk. bous.
kō eṉṉuñ col, āveṉṉum poruḷil tamiḻil vaḻaṅkāmaiyālum, āriyattil vaḻaṅkuvatiṉālum atai āriyaccol eṉṟu koḷḷaṟka. pacuvaik kuṟikka, ā, peṟṟam eṉa ēṉaiyiru coṟkaḷ vaḻaṅkuvatiṉālēyē, kō eṉṉum col ap poruḷil vaḻakkaṟṟateṉka.
kōvaiyuṭaiyavaṉ kōṉ. aṉ īṟṟil akaram tokkatu. o.nō: yāvar - yār.
kōvaṉ, kōṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ iyaṟporuḷil iṭaiyaṉaiyum, uruvakap poruḷil aracaṉaiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
kōvaṉirai mīṭṭaṉaṉ (cīvaka. 455) eṉpatil iṭaiyaṉaiyum, kōvaṉum makkaḷum (cīvaka. 1843) eṉpatil aracaṉaiyum kōvaṉ eṉṉuñ col kuṟittatu.
kōṉ kōṉār (uyarvuppaṉmai) eṉṉum peyarkaḷ iṭaiyarkkuk kuṭippeyarāy vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. kōṉ eṉṉum col, ceṅkōṉ kaṭuṅkōṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷil 
aracaṉaik kuṟittatu.
aracaṉ eṉṉum poruḷil, kōṉ eṉṉum peyarē īṟu keṭṭuk kō eṉṟākum. talaikkaḻakak kālat taracar peyar ceṅkōṉ kaṭuṅkōṉ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkiṉataiyum, kaṭaikkaḻakak kālattaracar peyar (pālaipāṭiya peruṅ) kaṭuṅkō, iḷaṅkō (aṭikaḷ) eṉṟu vaḻaṅkiṉataiyum, kō eṉṉum peyar pacupporuḷuk kēṟpataiyum kōṉ eṉṉum peyar aracaṉukkum iṭaiyaṉukkumaṉṟip pacuvuk kēlāmaiyaiyum nōkkuka.
kō eṉpatai, ā mā eṉpavaṟṟōṭu cērttu,
āmā kōṉav vaṇaiyavum peṟumē	(naṉ. 248)
eṉṟu pavaṇantiyār kūṟiyatu tavaṟākum.
kō eṉṉum peyar peṟṟattiṟku vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉamaiyiṉ, kōṉ eṉpatiṉ īṟukeṭṭa vaṭivam, 
aracaṉaik kuṟikkumpōtu mayakkattiṟ kiṭamillai.
tā eṉṉum col āriya moḻikaḷil vaḻaṅkiṉum, tamiḻukku eṅṅaṉam uriyatō, aṅṅaṉamē kō eṉṉuñ collum uriya teṉka. iṉṉum itaṉ mayakkut teḷiya, ip puttakattiṉ iṟutiyiṟ kāṇka.
araciyalpaṟṟic cila tamiḻccoṟkaḷ āriya moḻikaḷil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. avai aracu, pāḻi mutaliyaṉa. aracu eṉṉuñ colliṉ varalāṟu piṉṉark kūṟappaṭum.
pāḻi = nakar. Gk. polis, a city. ic colliliruntē, police (the system of regulations of a city), policy (manner of governing a city or 
nation), political (pertaining to policy), politics (science of government), 
polity (civil constitution) mutaliya coṟkaḷ piṟakkum.
toḻiṟperukkamum kulap pirivum
marutanilattil mutalāvatu uḻavar eṉṉum orē vakuppār iruntaṉar. piṉpu, muṟaiyē vaṇikam araciyal tuṟavu eṉpaṉa paṟṟi, av vakuppiṉiṉṟum pirintu pōṉavar, vāṇikar aracar antaṇar eṉappaṭṭār. uḻavar mutaliya nāṟpālum piṟkālat til ēṟṟattalaimai muṟaiyil talaimāṟṟik kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. uḻavar kaṭaiyiṟ kūṟappaṭṭamaiyiṉ kaṭaiyar eṉappaṭṭār.
kol, necavu mutaliya kaittoḻilpaṟṟip piṉpu uḻavar kulattiṉiṉṟē palar pirintaṉar.
uḻavar piṟarainōkka, vēḷāṇmaiyil (upacārattil) ciṟantiruntamaiyiṉ vēḷāḷar eṉappaṭṭār. avaruḷ vaṟiya rāyiṉār uḻutuṇpārum celvarāyiṉār uḻuvittuṇpāru māyiṉar. ivarē niṟampaṟṟi muṟaiyē karuṅkaḷamar allatu kārāḷar eṉṟum, veṇkaḷamar allatu veḷḷāḷar eṉṟuṅ kūṟappaṭupavar. kaḷattil vēlai ceypavar kaḷamar.
karuṅkaḷamarum oḻukkam, ūṇ, iṭam, paḻakkavaḻakkam mutaliyaṉapaṟṟip piṟkālattiṟ paṟpala kulamāyp pirintu pōyiṉar. iṅṅaṉamē piṟakulattārum. ivaṟṟiṉ virivai eṉatu teṉṉāṭṭuk kulamarapu eṉṉum nūliṟ kaṇṭukoḷka.
nakarattil araciyal tōṉṟiṉapiṉ tiṇaimayakkam ēṟpaṭṭatu. mullainilattilirunta iṭaiyarum pālainilatti lirunta kaḷḷar maṟavarum, neytalnilattilirunta cempaṭavarum, kuṟiñcinilattilirunta kuṟavarum nakarattiṟku vantu tattam toḻilaic ceyvārāyiṉar. avaruṭ kaḷḷarum maṟavarum muṟaiyē cōḻaṉukkum pāṇṭiyaṉukkum paṭaiñarāyiṉar. paṇṭait tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ veṟṟic ciṟappiṟku ivviru kulamum peruṅkāraṇameṉpatu teṉṉāṭṭuk kulamarapil viḷakkap paṭum.
kaḷḷar taṉit tamiḻarāyiruppavum, avaraip pallavareṉṟu  ōr  ayal  vakuppārākak  kūṟi varukiṉṟaṉar cilar. pallavar eṉpār,  ki.pi. 4ām nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal 9ām nūṟṟāṇṭu varaiyum,  cōḻanāṭṭai  allatu  toṇṭai  maṇṭalattai  yāṇṭa ōr aracak kuṭumpattārēyaṉṟit taṉik kulattiṉarallar. avaruṭaiya  kuṭikaḷum  paṭaiñarum tamiḻarē. avarukkut taṉimoḻiyum taṉimatamumillai. tamiḻnāṭṭu moḻikaḷum mataṅkaḷumē avarkkiruntaṉavum. celvamum maṟamum paṭaitta evaṉum, oru paṭaiyait tiraṭṭikkoṇṭu oru nāṭṭaik kaippaṟṟa eṉṟum iṭamuṇṭu.
pallavarkkirunta paṭṭap peyarkaḷuḷ, toṇṭaiyaṉ eṉpatum oṉṟu. pallavarkkum paṉṉūṟṟāṇṭukku muṉpē, cōḻanāṭṭiṉ vaṭapākattiṟkut toṇṭaimaṇṭalam eṉṟum, ataṉ aracaṉukkut toṇṭaimāṉ eṉṟum peyar vaḻaṅkiṉamai perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭaiyā laṟiyappaṭum. av vāṟṟuppaṭait talaivaṉaip pāṭiya kaṭiyalūr uruttiraṅ kaṇṇaṉār, karikāl vaḷavaṉaiyum pāṭiyuḷḷār. karikāl vaḷavaṉ kālam ki.pi. 1ām nūṟṟāṇṭākum. ākaiyāl, putukkōṭṭai aracarait toṇṭaimāṉ eṉṉum paṭṭam paṟṟip pallava marapiṉarākak kūṟutal poruntāteṉka. mēlum toṇṭaimāṉ eṉpatu taṉittamiḻc collātaluṅ kāṇka.
vaḻipāṭum matamum
vaḻipāṭāvatu oru ciṟuteyvattaiyēṉum muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷaiyēṉum vaṇaṅkum vaṇakkam. matamāvatu vīṭupēṟu karuti muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷai yaṭaiyum peruneṟi.
mati + am = matam. matittal - karututal. kaṭavuḷaippaṟṟiya matippu matamākum. matattiṟkuc camayam eṉṟum peyaruṇṭu. 
camai + am = (camaiyam) - camayam. camaital - pakkuvamātal. peṇṭir pūppaṭaitalaiyum arici cōṟātalaiyum camaital eṉṟu colluvatum, pakkuvamātal eṉṉuṅ karuttuppaṟṟiyē. matam āṉmākkaḷai vīṭupēṟṟiṟkup pakkuvappaṭuttalāl camayam eṉappaṭṭatu. camaiyam = pakkuvamāṉa vēḷai, vēḷai. camayam pakkuvamākkum neṟi allatu koḷkai. vēḷaiyaik kuṟikkum camaiyam eṉṉum col, matattaik kuṟittaṟku aikāram akaramāyiṟṟu. oru col poruḷ vēṟupaṭutaṟku eḻuttu māṟuvatu, oru colliyal nūl neṟimuṟaiyākum.
kā :	paḻaimai (toṉmai) - paḻamai (purāṇam); mutaliyār - mutaliyōr.
	noṭippaḻamai, paḻamai pēcutal eṉṉum vaḻakku kaḷai nōkkuka.
vaḻipāṭu - Cults
aintiṇait teyvam
māyōṉ mēya kāṭuṟai yulakamum
 cēyōṉ mēya maivarai ulakamum
 vēntaṉ mēya tīmpuṉal ulakamum
 varuṇaṉ mēya perumaṇal ulakamum
 mullai kuṟiñci marutam neytaleṉac
 colliya muṟaiyāṟ collavum paṭumē	(akat. 5)
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam.
mullaitteyvam māyōṉ
mā = karuppu. māyōṉ kariyōṉ. māyōṉukku māl eṉṟum peyar. māl = karuppu, mēkam, vāṉam, kariyōṉ. mullainilattil, mēlē eṅkup pārttālum nīla allatu kariya vāṉamum mēkamumāyt tōṉṟuvatālum, āniraikku vēṇṭiya pullum āyarkku vēṇṭiya vāṉa vāri allatu puṉceyp payirkaḷum vaḷarvataṟku maḻai vēṇṭiyiruppatālum, mēkattai vāṉattōṭoppak koṇṭatiṉālum, mullai nilattār taṅkaḷ teyvattaik karumaiyāṉateṉṟu karuti, māyōṉ eṉṟum māl eṉṟum peyariṭṭaṉar. tirumāl eṉpatil tiru eṉpatu aṭai.
mullainilattiṟkuriya kaluḻaṉai(karuṭaṉai)yum tuḷaciyai yum, muṟaiyē māyōṉukkuriya ūrtiyākavum pūvākavum koṇṭārkaḷ.
kuṟiñcitteyvam cēyōṉ
cēyōṉ = civantavaṉ. cēyōṉ cēntaṉ civaṉ eṉpaṉa oruporuṭ coṟkaḷ. kuṟiñciyil mūṅkilāl aṭikkaṭi tīppaṟṟik koṇṭatiṉālum, tī añcattakkatāṉa tiṉālum. atait (teyvamāka allatu) teyva veḷip pāṭākak koṇṭu, ataṟkuc cēyōṉ eṉṟu peyariṭṭārkaḷ.
cēyōṉukkuk kuṟiñci nilattiṟkuriya mayilai ūrti yākavum, cēvalaik koṭiyākavum, kaṭampamalaraip pūvākavuṅ koṇṭaṉar.
kōḻiyai vīṭṭil vaḷarttatu piṟkāla vaḻakkam. cūrapaṉmaṉ mayiluṅ kōḻiyumākik kantaṉukku muṟaiyē ūrtiyuṅ koṭiyumāṉāṉ eṉṟatu piṟkālak katai. cēval pōr ceyvatiṟ ciṟanta paṟavaiyātalāl, at toḻiliṟ ciṟanta cēyōṉukkuk koṭiyuruvamāyk koḷḷappaṭṭateṉka.
 cēyōṉukku murukaṉ, kantaṉ, āṟumukaṉ eṉṟum peyaruṇṭu. muruku eṉpatu maṇam, aḻaku, iḷamai eṉṉum pala poruḷkaḷai yuṭaiyatu. teyvattiṟku allatu pēykku maṇam viruppameṉṟum, malaraṇintukoṇṭāvatu malaruḷḷa iṭattilāvatu paruvappeṇkaḷ taṉiccellak kūṭāteṉṟum, oru koḷkai tamiḻarkkuḷ iṉṟum iruntuvarukiṉṟatu.
teyvam iyalpāka aḻakuḷḷateṉṟu ellā matattā rālum oppukkoḷḷappaṭukiṉṟatu. itaṉāl aḻakaṉ eṉṟu tirumālukkum cokkaṉ eṉṟu civaṉukkum piṟkālattiṟ peyarkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. aḻaku eppōtum iḷamaiyoṭu kūṭiyatu. kōvaiyil nilaikaṇṭuraittal eṉṉun tuṟaiyai nōkkuka.
kantaṉ eṉṉum peyar, kantil (tūṇil) teyvavuruvaip poṟitta allatu cetukkiya piṟkālattil tōṉṟiṉatākat terikiṉṟatu. kantiṟpāvai eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka. kantaṉaiyē skantaṉ eṉṟaṉar vaṭanūlār.
āṟumukam eṉṉum peyar piṉṉark kūṟappaṭum.
marutatteyvam vēntaṉ
vēntaṉ =  aracaṉ.
nalviṉai ceytavariṉ uyirkaḷ iṟantapiṉ mēlulakat tiṟkuc cellumeṉṟum, ulakil (marutanilattil) aracaṉāyirun tavaṉ maṟumaiyil mēlulakattilum aracaṉāvāṉ eṉṟum, marutanila māntar karuti, mutaṉmutal iṟanta aracaṉaiyē vēntaṉ eṉṟu peyariṭṭu vaṇaṅkiṉārkaḷ. maḻai mēliruntu peyvatāl, mēlulaka vēntaṉākiya taṅkaḷ teyvattiṉiṭamiruntē varuvatākak karuti, maḻaivaḷattiṟkākavum avaṉai vaḻipaṭṭārkaḷ.
nalviṉaiyāvaṉa vēḷāṇmaiyum pōrttoḻilum. pōrttoḻilil oruvaṉ piṟar naṉmaikkeṉṟu taṉ uyiraik koṭuttalāl, atu talaiciṟanta vēḷāṇmaiyākum. ciṟanta illaṟattārkkum pōril paṭṭa maṟavarkkum maṟumaiyil vāṉulakam eṉpatu, avar vēḷāṇmaiyiṟ ciṟantavar eṉṉum karuttuppaṟṟiyē,
celvirun tōmpi varuviruntu pārttiruppāṉ
 nalviruntu vāṉat tavarkku	(kuṟaḷ. 86)

nīḷkaḻaṉ maṟavar celvuḻic celka	(puṟat. 93)
eṉpataṉāl, pōriliṟantavar vāṉulakam pukuvar eṉṟa koḷ kai yaṟiyappaṭum.
uḻavuttoḻil ceyyum paḷḷarum pōrttoḻiṟkuriya maṟavarum, iṉṟum taṅkaḷai intirakulattāreṉṟum, taṅkaḷ kulamutalvaṉ intiraṉeṉṟum kūṟikkoḷkiṉṟaṉar.
aracaṉiṭattil vēḷāṇmaiyum pōrttoḻilum oruṅkēyuṇṭu. avaṉ immaiyilum maḻaikkuk kāraṇamākak karutappaṭṭāṉ.

eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
vēḷāṇmaikkuc ciṟanta uḻavar kuṭiyiruppatum, ciṟanta aracu mutaṉmutal tōṉṟiyatum marutanilamē.
pālainilattu maṟavar paṭaiñarākumuṉ, marutanilattu uḻavarē pōrttoḻil ceytu vantaṉar. ataṉ piṉpum uḻavar pōrttoḻilai viṭṭuviṭavillai. itai,
vēntuviṭu toḻiliṟ paṭaiyum kaṇṇiyum
 vāyntaṉar eṉpa avarpeṟum poruḷē	(marapu. 77)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyaṅ kūṟuvatālum, maḷḷar, maḻavar eṉṉum peyarkaṭku uḻavar maṟavar eṉṉu miru poruḷu muṇmaiyālum, uḻavar kulattil oru vakuppār paṭaiyāṭciyeṉṟu peyar peṟṟiruppatiṉālum, mēṉāṭṭilum kīḻnāṭṭilumirunta paṇṭai amaranāyaka (Feudalism)KiwahY« aṟiyalām.
paṇṭaik kālattil aracaraiyum teyvamāka vaṇaṅkiṉar eṉpatu, iraṇiyaṉ, nēpukāttēccār mutaliyōr carittirat tālum, aracaṉum teyvamu mirukkumiṭam kōyil eṉṟu kūṟappaṭuvatiṉālum, aracaṉukkum aracikkum tēvaṉ tēvi eṉṉum peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅkuvatālum, tiruvāyk kēḷvi tirumantira vōlai mutaliya aracaka aluvaṟ peyarkaḷālum, piṟavaṟṟālum aṟiyappaṭum.
vēntaṉ eṉṉum peyaraiyē, intiraṉ eṉṟu āriyar moḻipeyarttukkoṇṭaṉar. intiraṉ = aracaṉ. narēntiraṉ, mirukēntiraṉ, kavīntiraṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷai nōkkuka.
nūṟu kutiraivēḷvi vēntaṉ (intiraṉ) patavikkut takutiyāka āriyappaḻamainūl kūṟum. kutiraivēḷvi ceypavaṉ aracaṉē.
kaṭaikkaḻakak kālamvarai vēntaṉ vaḻipāṭu tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ ciṟantiruntateṉpatu, cilappatikārattālum maṇimēkalaiyālum piṟanūl kuṟippukaḷālum aṟiyappaṭum.
(āyarpāṭiyil vēntaṉ vaḻipāṭṭai niṟuttiya) kaṇṇaṉ vaḻipāṭu varavarat tamiḻnāṭṭil valuttatiṉālum, nakara māntaruḷ uḻavar ciṟupāṉmaiyāṉatiṉālum, vēntaṉ maḻaivaḷam oṉṟē tarum ciṟuteyvamātalālum, caivam tirumāliyam eṉṉum iraṇṭum vīṭupēṟṟiṟkuriya perumataṅkaḷāy vaḷarntuviṭṭamai yālum, vēntaṉ vaṇakkam perumpālum niṉṟuviṭṭatu. itupōtu orōvōriṭattuḷḷa maḻait teyvavuruvamē paṇṭai vēntaṉ vaḻipāṭṭiṉ aṭaiyāḷamāyuḷḷatu.
marutanilat teyvattukkup paṇṭirunta perumaiyai, akattiyar vēntaṉ civikaiyaic cumantatākat tiruviḷaiyāṭaṟ purāṇamum kumarimalaiyai makēntirameṉṟu vaṭanūlkaḷum aintirattai viṇṇavar kōmāṉ viḻunūl eṉṟu cilappati kāramum kūṟuvatum, vēntaṉ viḻāvai oru cōḻaṉ niṟuttiyatāl pukār kaṭalvāyppaṭṭateṉṟa koḷkaiyum viḷakkum.
vēntaṉukkuc cēṇōṉ purantaraṉ eṉṟum peyaruṇṭu. cēṇ uyaram. cēṇulakattaracaṉ cēṇōṉ. vāṉulakaip purantarupavaṉ purantaraṉ. purantarutal kāttal.
neytalnilat teyvam vāraṇaṉ
